### 2026-10-21 11:28:46  #MKT News #Commodities #Energy #WTI #Impact bullish #Brent

【Morgan Stanley warns global refined-fuel supply could remain tight through mid-2027】Morgan Stanley says even if current war-related supply disruptions ease, global refined-product markets could remain tight through mid-2027 as unusually large refinery maintenance is constraining supply. Analysts led by Martin Latz expect planned refinery outages through Q2 next year to remove about 3.4 mln b/d of capacity — roughly 2.5 times normal and larger than current reductions from Middle East and Russian refiners. Eight months of Middle East conflict and recent reciprocal attacks on energy infrastructure between Russia and Ukraine have exacerbated the shortfall. Diesel exports have dropped sharply, pushing diesel prices above crude; diesel is critical for transport, heating and industry. Morgan Stanley says many refineries delayed maintenance to chase margins but cannot defer it indefinitely, and run rates are unlikely to rebound materially by Q2 next year.

### 2026-10-21 11:14:31  #Trading Economics #Markets #Indonesia #Currency #Importance 1

Rupiah Holds Gains as BI Rate Decision Looms — The Indonesian rupiah hovered near IDR 17,970 per dollar on Wednesday, extending strength for a third session as the US dollar remained under pressure after the dollar index fell to a three-week low. Softer US Treasury yields and easing crude oil prices reduced support for the greenback, helping lift emerging market currencies, including rupiah. Traders awaited Bank Indonesia's policy decision later today, with markets widely expecting the central bank to hold its key rate at 5.75% for a fourth straight month following 100bps of cumulative tightening earlier this year. Sentiment was also aided by receding concerns over MSCI potentially reclassifying Indonesia as a standalone market, after regulators launched measures to address concentrated share ownership and strengthen shareholder disclosure requirements. However, the upside was capped by persistent fiscal concerns and renewed cost pressures, as El Niño-related food prices accelerated and September inflation hit a three-month high.

### 2026-10-21 11:04:26  #Trading Economics #Markets #Japan #Currency #Importance 1

Yen Holds Steady Amid Sluggish Export Growth — The Japanese yen was little changed around 158.2 per dollar on Wednesday, remaining within a narrow range as investors assessed data showing that exports increased less than expected in September and posted the slowest growth since April. Still, exports extended their growth streak to 13 consecutive months, supported by strong demand for semiconductors and AI-related products. On the monetary policy front, the Bank of Japan is widely expected to leave interest rates unchanged next week before implementing a rate hike in December. Earlier this week, Governor Kazuo Ueda noted that underlying inflation was nearing the central bank’s 2% target, reinforcing expectations of a more hawkish policy stance. However, the yen remained under pressure from persistent carry trades, as Japan’s relatively low interest rates continued to weigh on its appeal. The BOJ’s gradual approach to monetary tightening is also viewed as insufficient to close the interest rate gap with other major economies.

【中指研究院：10月京滬二手房市場成交保持活躍 剛需仍是成交主力】中指研究院發文稱，今年前三季度，北京二手住宅網籤137229套，同比增長5.1%，成交規模為2022年以來同期最高；上海二手住宅網籤191563套，同比增長15.5%，三季度以來，單月成交規模連續三個月創近五年同期新高。進入10月，二手房市場活躍度延續。截至10月20日，北京二手住宅、上海二手商品房分別成交7029套、14174套，同比增長25.9%、37.9%。整體來看，京滬二手房市場成交保持活躍，剛需仍是成交主力，改善性需求出現邊際恢復。但兩地價格表現有所分化：上海二手房掛牌價格趨於穩定，北京掛牌價格仍有下行壓力。後續改善性需求能否持續釋放，以及成交回升能否進一步帶動價格企穩，仍值得關注。

### 2026-10-21 11:03:23  #Trading Economics #Markets #Commodity #Importance 1

US Natgas Prices Edge Higher — US natural gas prices edged up to $2.97 per MMBtu on Wednesday, attempting to rebound from a four-week low hit in the previous session, as improving LNG export activity and reduced output partly offset weak demand due to mild weather. Average gas flows to the nine major US LNG export terminals rose to 18.2 bcfd so far in October from 17.9 bcfd in September. Meanwhile, gas production across the Lower 48 states averaged 111.4 bcfd this month, down from record highs of 113.3 bcfd in August and September. The storage surplus also continued to shrink, with inventories as of October 9 standing 3.9% below year-earlier levels and just 1.3% above the five-year average. Meanwhile, mild temperatures are expected to persist through late October, curbing both heating and cooling demand, while expectations of a “Super El Niño” further weigh on the winter demand outlook.

### 2026-10-21 11:02:58  #公司 #市場 #觀點

【瑞銀：降寧德時代目標價至730港元，單位利潤因產品結構及退稅變動而承壓】瑞銀髮表研報指，寧德時代第三季淨利潤為212億元，同比增長15%，但環比下跌6%，較買方預期低約10%。首九個月淨利潤為645億元，僅達全年市場共識與該行預估的62%至67%，這可能導致市場對全年共識預期的下修。若排除非經常性專案，第三季淨利潤為189億元，同比增15%，但環比降10%。期內電池銷量超過260GWh，當中儲能系統(ESS)佔約25%，電動車電池佔約75%。該行指出，按此計算的單位利潤降至每千瓦時82元，創歷史新低，主要受季度產品組合波動、低增值專案增加、9月1日起對鋰離子電池徵收消費稅，以及出口增值稅退稅率由9%降至6%影響。不過，管理層對2027年行業20%至30%的增長前景仍保持信心。瑞銀相應將該公司2026至2028年淨利潤預測下調11%至14%，降至介乎900億至1310億元，目標價由820港元下調至730港元，維持“買入”評級。

### 2026-10-21 11:02:50  #Trading Economics #Economy #New Zealand #Calendar #Importance 1

New Zealand Credit Card Spending Rises 6.9% in September — Seasonally adjusted total billings in New Zealand rose 6.9% year-on-year to NZD 4.7 billion in September 2026, following an upwardly revised 3.6% increase in August. It was the seventh consecutive month of growth, driven by higher domestic billings on cards issued in New Zealand, which climbed 5.3% to NZD 4.0 billion, while overseas billings grew 3.9%. Meanwhile, total credit limits edged down to NZD 20.8 billion, while credit limit utilisation increased to 28.7% at the end of September. The weighted average effective interest rate on personal interest-bearing advances remained at 20.0% in August 2026.

【證監會商慶軍：促進北交所新三板一體化高質量發展 三方面持續發力】中國證監會公眾公司監管司一級巡視員商慶軍10月21日在2026金融街論壇年會“共建服務中小企業資本市場新生態”平行論壇上表示，證監會將深入貫徹落實黨中央、國務院決策部署，堅持穩中求進總基調，以防風險、強監管、促發展為主線，持續健全監管制度體系，促進北交所與新三板一體化高質量發展，更好服務“十五五”規劃落地和金融強國建設。商慶軍介紹，下一步將重點從三方面推進工作：一是厚植優質企業創新發展基礎。立足服務創新型中小企業定位，釋出北交所創新性評價標準，精準識別各類創新要素，明確申報預期，為處於行業前沿、具有創新能力、持續增長潛力和專業化發展優勢的創新型中小企業提供更好服務，持續最佳化上市公司結構。同時，強化新三板規範培育功能，健全准入和退出機制，最佳化分層管理，增強創新層品牌效應和融資配置功能，完善差異化資訊披露安排，鞏固北交所與新三板“層層遞進、上下聯動、頭部引領、底層助推”的發展格局，為專精特新企業提供持續接力、精準識別的資本市場服務，更好促進新質生產力發展。二是最佳化投資生態，增強市場內在韌性。長期資金是市場“壓艙石”和“穩定器”。下一步將加大投資端建設力度，統籌促進一二級市場平衡發展，健全投資和融資相協調的資本市場功能。一方面，完善北交所發行承銷制度，穩妥實施新股市值配售，提升二級市場投資者獲得感，促進新股合理定價，持續推動詢價發行案例落地，進一步提升定價彈性；另一方面，加快建設市場產品體系，加大公募基金供給力度，穩步推出北證50ETF，促進投融資兩端平衡發展。同時，完善風險監測預警和應急處置機制，切實維護市場秩序，保護投資者合法權益。三是堅持嚴監嚴管，提升上市公司質量。依託上市公司治理專項行動，督促上市中小企業提升公司治理水平；支援獨立董事、審計委員會主動、有效履職，調動機構投資者和中小股東參與公司治理積極性，強化公司內部監督與制衡。加強對大股東、實際控制人的規範約束，緊盯上市公司“關鍵少數”，對欺詐發行、財務造假、資金佔用等嚴重侵害投資者合法權益的行為及時採取行動，嚴厲打擊重大違法行為，堅決肅清違法違規亂象。（上證報）

### 2026-10-21 10:47:08  #其他

【新方法可引導合成分子形成特定三維結構】芬蘭研究人員開發出一種新方法，可透過在人工合成分子中編碼不同層級的資訊，引導其自組裝形成特定的三維結構。這一成果有望為開發智慧材料和分子尺度器件等提供新思路。 在生物體中，蛋白質等分子能夠根據其分子序列中編碼的資訊，自發形成特定的三維結構。然而如何讓人工合成分子也能如此精確地形成預定結構，一直是化學研究面臨的一大挑戰。 芬蘭于韋斯屈萊大學研究人員開發出一種分級的序列控制方法，透過在分子中編碼兩級資訊，分步控制其自組裝過程。第一級資訊進行初步選擇，將分子的可能結構限定為4種型別的螺旋。第二級資訊進一步引導在這4種螺旋中形成一種特定螺旋：逆時針三螺旋，即三條分子鏈相互纏繞、逆時針盤旋。與此同時，這種分級控制還會有效抑制其他可能結構的形成。 研究表明，透過對分子序列進行精細調整，可以精確控制分子最終形成的三維結構。研究團隊希望進一步提高對分子自組裝過程的預測和調控能力，使人工合成分子能夠按照預先設計的方式，形成更加複雜的三維結構。 相關研究成果已發表在學術期刊《應用化學國際版》上。(新華社)

---

Indonesian Shares Retreat Ahead of BI Rate Call — Indonesian equities fell 84 points, or 1.6%, to 5,197 on Wednesday morning, reversing a prior rally after regulatory measures aimed at regaining investor confidence, including adjustments to stocks with high shareholding concentration and greater disclosure of shareholder affiliations. Traders turned cautious ahead of Bank Indonesia's rate decision later today. While the central bank is expected to hold borrowing costs for a fourth month, room for easing is limited after a cumulative 100bps hike in May and June. Still, the decline was capped by record FDI in Q3 2026, lifted by solid inflows into the base metal industry. In top trading partner China, key lending rates were left unchanged for a 17th month after stronger-than-expected Q3 GDP. Cyclicals, basic materials, and financials were mainly lower, partly offset by strength in infrastructure, non-cyclicals, and transport. Notable decliners included Solusi Energi Digital (-6.9%), Mitra Adiperkasa (-6.0%), and Medco Energi (-3.9%).

Taiwan Stocks Rebound from Near 5-Week Low — The TAIEX, Taiwan’s benchmark stock index, advanced 860 points, or 1.9%, to 47,153 in Wednesday morning trade, erasing losses from the previous two sessions and tracking gains on Wall Street overnight amid easing Treasury yields and oil prices. 【寧德時代回應“投資者對明年行業需求擔憂”：2027年行業需求依舊可期 沒有市場預期的那麼悲觀】近期部分投資者對明年新能源行業需求有些擔心。對此，寧德時代回應稱，從公司角度看，2027年行業需求依舊可以期待。國內乘用車方面，儘管今年乘用車整體銷量有所下降，但可以看到電動化率在快速提升，並且單車帶電量有較快提升，所以國內新能源乘用車動力電池的增速依然實現了正增長。國內新能源商用車的電動化率也在快速提升；歐洲的電動化趨勢具有連續性，不斷有競爭力的新車型推出；其他市場的電動化潛力可能被市場低估。儲能方面，無論是國內還是海外都保持著不錯的增速，特別是海外部分市場的儲能需求比較旺盛。整體而言，全球動力及儲能電池的市場需求可能沒有市場預期的那麼悲觀，仍有不錯的增長潛力。

【黃河干流寧夏段連續9年保持Ⅱ類水質】記者20日從寧夏生態環境廳獲悉，“十四五”以來，寧夏不斷加強水生態環境保護與修復，全區水生態環境質量持續穩定向好。截至2025年底，黃河干流寧夏段已連續9年穩定保持Ⅱ類水質，地表水國控斷面Ⅲ類及以上優良水質比例達到85%，國家監測的15個城市集中式飲用水水源地水質全部達標並保持優良。 近年來，寧夏強化重點斷面水質管控，實時監測水質變化，採取針對性防控措施，確保水質穩定達標。持續開展排汙口排查整治，累計排查各類排汙口4682個，逐項整改銷號，整治率達到97.1%。透過實施重點入黃排水溝生態治理工程，全區22條重點入黃排水溝水質全部達到或優於地表水Ⅳ類標準。全面推行河湖長制以來，寧夏累計規範整治236處黃河寧夏段“亂佔、亂建”問題。 另外，寧夏推進汙水處理提質增效，切實鞏固黑臭水體治理成效。目前，全區已建成城市生活汙水處理廠全部達到一級A排放標準，城市生活汙水處理率、收集率分別達到99.15%、79.8%。全區地級城市建成區13條黑臭水體已全部消除，未出現返黑返臭；12個縣城建成區排查未發現黑臭水體。(新華社)

【壁仞科技魏明：算力閒置的另一面，是高階算力“非常缺”】2026可持續全球領導者大會於10月20日-22日在上海黃浦世博園區舉辦。在《以東方力量推進高質量發展》行業主題論壇上，壁仞科技戰略規劃高階副總裁魏明指出，算力市場呈現明顯的結構性矛盾：一方面部分算力處於閒置狀態，另一方面高階算力卻“非常缺”。“如果問大模型公司今年最挑戰的事情是什麼，那就是缺算力，缺高階算力。”魏明表示。他介紹，目前國產高階晶片的算力已經起來，能夠提供相應服務，這為緩解高階算力緊缺提供了現實支撐。與此同時，閒置算力也可透過PD分離等方式盤活——把算力較強的國產卡與其他視訊記憶體較強卡配合使用，讓不同晶片各展所長，以新技術逐步化解閒置問題。在魏明看來，隨著國產晶片能力提升與排程技術不斷成熟，閒置與緊缺並存的矛盾有望逐步緩解，他對國產卡的應用前景充滿信心。

European Natural Gas Rebounds — European natural gas prices rose to €87/MWh on Wednesday, moving back near a multi-year high after sliding more than 3% in the previous session as traders weighed mixed reports on efforts to end the Middle East war. Tuesday’s decline came after unconfirmed reports that Qatar and Pakistan had submitted a new proposal aimed at reviving talks to end the conflict. However, a diplomat involved in the negotiations said reports of an imminent agreement were incorrect. European gas prices remain more than double their pre-conflict levels amid ongoing disruptions to LNG supplies from the Persian Gulf and unusually low inventories. As of October 20, European storage facilities were 74.62% full, the lowest seasonal level since records began in 2009. The situation is particularly concerning in Germany, Europe’s largest economy, with storage levels just above 61%. Strong LNG demand from Asia also increases the risk of more intense competition for cargoes when colder weather arrives.

【黔西南州城市建設投資集團原總經理潘維維被“雙開”】經貴州省委批准，貴州省紀委監委對黔西南州城市建設投資（集團）有限公司原黨委副書記、總經理潘維維嚴重違紀違法問題進行了立案審查調查。經查，潘維維喪失理想信念，背棄初心使命，政績觀扭曲錯位，背離高質量發展要求；違反中央八項規定精神，接受可能影響公正執行公務的宴請；背離組織原則，個人或少數人決定重大問題；不正確履行職責，造成不良影響；目無法紀，挪用公款歸個人使用；利用職務便利為他人謀取利益，並非法收受鉅額財物。潘維維嚴重違反黨的政治紀律、組織紀律、廉潔紀律、工作紀律和生活紀律，構成嚴重職務違法並涉嫌挪用公款、受賄犯罪，且在黨的十八大後不收斂、不收手，性質嚴重，影響惡劣，應予嚴肅處理。依據《中國共產黨紀律處分條例》《中華人民共和國監察法》《中華人民共和國公職人員政務處分法》等有關規定，經貴州省紀委常委會會議研究並報貴州省委批准，決定給予潘維維開除黨籍處分；由貴州省監委給予其開除公職處分，終止其中國共產黨黔西南州第八次代表大會代表資格，將其涉嫌犯罪問題移送檢察機關依法審查起訴，所涉財物一併移送。

Heating Oil Moves Toward Record — US heating oil futures rose to around $5 per gallon, moving closer to the record high touched in mid-September, after industry data showed a further inventory draw. API data showed that US distillate stocks fell by 2.6 million barrels in the week ending October 16, underscoring tight supplies. Morgan Stanley also warned that oil-product markets could remain tight through mid-2027, as planned refinery outages are expected to rise by 3.4 million barrels per day through Q2 2027, about 2.5 times the norm. Meanwhile, Middle East supply concerns persisted, despite Gulf oil flows remaining more resilient than feared. Traders also weighed the potential for increased Russian fuel exports after President Trump waived new Russia-related sanctions and tariffs. Secretary of State Marco Rubio said the administration could take up to 60 days to assess their economic impact, leaving uncertainty over whether the move would help ease global fuel supply pressures.

China Stocks Rise on Tech Optimism — The Shanghai Composite edged up 0.2% to 3,742, while the Shenzhen Component gained 0.6% to 11,870, as upbeat corporate earnings lifted sentiment toward technology stocks. SpaceX reported net income well above market expectations, while Super Micro Computer also delivered better-than-expected results, helping ease concerns over stretched AI valuations and funding pressures that recently rattled the sector. However, sluggish domestic investment and consumption continue to pose risks to growth in the absence of broader policy support.

【中國電科金剛石異質結獲突破 為半導體材料製備提供新正規化】中國電科產業基礎研究院固態微波器件與電路全國重點實驗室金剛石團隊，在金剛石異質結取得突破，打造出首個立方相氮化鋁/金剛石異質結材料和場效應電晶體。科研團隊創新提出疇匹配外延生長模式，利用金剛石表面氫終端當作調控反應的平臺，形成高質量單晶立方氮化鋁/金剛石異質結，併成功研製出高效能增強型異質結場效應電晶體。這項工作完成了從理論計算到材料製備，再到器件驗證的關鍵銜接，為高效能金剛石射頻器件提供了材料基礎。團隊開發的異質結外延方法為半導體材料製備提供新正規化。下一步，團隊將繼續最佳化材料與器件工藝，推動異質結技術發展，支撐新一代超寬禁帶半導體的技術升級。

【中國證監會：調動機構投資者、中小股東參與北交所公司治理積極性】10月21日，中國證監會公眾公司監管司一級巡視員商慶軍在2026金融街論壇年會上表示，上市公司是資本市場的基石，打造高質量上市公司群體，有利於改善市場生態、最佳化資源配置、增強投資者信心，更好服務實體經濟發展。北交所主要服務創新型中小企業，部分上市公司仍處於發展早期，需要在提升公司治理水平、保障規範成長方面下更多功夫。一方面，我們將依託上市公司治理專項行動，督促上市中小企業練好自我提升的“內功”，支援獨立董事、審計委員會主動、有效履職，調動機構投資者、中小股東參與公司治理的積極性，強化公司內部監督與制衡。另一方面，我們還將加強對大股東、實控人的規範約束，緊盯上市公司“關鍵少數”，對欺詐發行、財務造假、資金佔用等嚴重侵害中小投資者利益行為及時出手、嚴厲打擊，觸及重大違法退市標準的堅決出清。

### 2026-10-21 09:51:15  #Trading Economics #Markets #Australia #Government Bond 10Y #Importance 1

AUS 10Y Yield Trades Near Multi-Week Lows — Australia’s 10-year government bond yield held around 5.2%, trading close to multi-week lows as investors continued to assess developments on the monetary policy outlook. RBA Deputy Governor Andrew Hauser said tighter monetary policy was working, but warned that the inflation outlook remained “materially worse” partly due to elevated oil prices. Hauser stressed the need to anchor inflation expectations ahead of the RBA’s November 3 policy meeting, where policymakers will consider whether to raise rates for a fifth time this year. However, he also acknowledged that higher interest rates and fuel costs were slowing the economy. Markets subsequently raised the probability of a November hike to 44% chance from 30% at the start of the week, while the odds of a December move had climbed to 64%. Focus now turns to third-quarter inflation data due next week. Core inflation is expected to rise 1.0% from 0.9% previously, keeping the annual pace at 3.5%, well above the RBA’s target range.

China Youth Jobless Rate Eases to 18% — China's urban youth unemployment rate for people aged 16–24, excluding students, eased to 18% in September 2026 from 18.9% in August, when it reached its highest level in 12 months. The National Bureau of Statistics attributed the monthly decline to graduates gradually securing jobs after the seasonal increase in unemployment during the July–August graduation period. Meanwhile, the jobless rate for people aged 25–29 edged down to 7.4% from 7.5%, while the rate for those aged 30–59 remained unchanged at 3.9%. The overall urban unemployment rate also eased to 5.2% from 5.3%, indicating a modest improvement in labor market conditions.

South Korean Shares Rebound on Chip Rally — The benchmark KOSPI rose nearly 4% to around 5,770 on Wednesday, recovering from a six-month closing low, as semiconductor stocks rallied. Samsung Electronics and SK hynix climbed more than 5% and 6%, respectively. Additionally, South Korea’s exports surged 105.4% year-on-year to $61.6 billion in the first 20 days of October, the highest for the period, driven by a 222.5% jump in semiconductor shipments. Investors also awaited earnings from Meta and other major technology companies for clues on AI spending and returns amid concerns over infrastructure investment. Separately, KB Securities estimated that Samsung Electronics had secured long-term supply agreements covering around 80% of its planned 2027 memory output, suggesting sustained demand despite recent market volatility. Other notable gainers included SK Square (5.8%), SK Inc. (9.4%), LS Electric (5.2%), Doosan Corporation (6.5%), Samsung C&T (5.6%), and SK Innovation (2.2%).

Silver Slips on Rising Oil Prices — Silver fell toward $57.5 an ounce on Wednesday, paring gains from the previous session as rising oil prices intensified inflation concerns and strengthened expectations for interest rate hikes. Oil resumed its upward trend after a diplomat dismissed reports that a deal to end the US-Iran conflict was imminent, although negotiations remain underway. Rising tensions in the Middle East and Eastern Europe, along with increased attacks on tankers in the Strait of Hormuz, further heightened concerns over supply disruptions. Meanwhile, the US Federal Reserve is widely expected to leave interest rates unchanged next week, though markets anticipate a possible rate hike in December. The Bank of Japan and the European Central Bank are also expected to maintain their policy rates next week. Although metals are traditionally considered a hedge against inflation, higher interest rates tend to weaken their appeal compared with interest-bearing assets.

### 2026-10-21 09:26:29  #Trading Economics #Economy #Bulgaria #Current Account #Importance 0

Bulgaria Current Account Swings to Deficit — Bulgaria's current account recorded a deficit of EUR 96 million in August 2026, shifting from a surplus of EUR 245.6 million in the same month a year earlier. The deterioration was driven by a wider goods shortfall, which increased to EUR 535.3 million from EUR 463.4 million, while the services surplus narrowed to EUR 970.5 million from EUR 1,107.7 million. At the same time, the primary income gap widened to EUR 688.6 million from EUR 464.2 million. In contrast, the secondary income surplus increased to EUR 157.4 million from EUR 65.5 million. Meanwhile, the combined goods and services balance remained in surplus at EUR 435.2 million, down from EUR 644.3 million a year earlier. In January-August 2026, the current account recorded a deficit of EUR 5,552.4 million, compared with a deficit of EUR 2,225.1 million in the corresponding period of 2025. Over the twelve months ending August 2026, the deficit widened to EUR 10,379.4 million from EUR 4,036.5 million a year earlier.

Global Dairy Prices Post Biggest Gain in 7 Months — The Global Dairy Trade Price Index rose 2.5% in the two weeks ended October 20, extending the previous auction’s 1.2% increase, according to GlobalDairyTrade. The latest result marked the strongest rise since early March, while the average winning price climbed to $4,021 per metric ton. Prices rose for buttermilk powder (+8.4%), whole milk powder (+4.4%), skim milk powder (+3.4%), mozzarella (+1.3%), and lactose (+0.6%). These gains were partly offset by declines in cheddar (-7.4%), anhydrous milk fat (-3.4%), and butter (-0.4%).

【Wall Street Journal: Failed German airport attack an 'opening salvo' in Russia's sabotage campaign against Europe】The Wall Street Journal reports that in early August a large Antonov cargo plane parked at an eastern German airport — previously used to transport military aid to Ukraine — was struck in an attack by explosive-laden drones. German officials say the drones were controlled by Russian military intelligence; one hit the Antonov but failed to detonate, a second struck another aircraft, and a third exploded in the airport perimeter. Investigators said the attack, had it succeeded, could have caused major disruption and casualties at one of Europe’s key air-freight hubs. A European official familiar with the intelligence said U.S. agencies assess the attempted strike at Leipzig-Halle Airport this summer is among the 'opening acts' of an escalating campaign of sabotage and covert operations ordered directly by Putin against NATO states.

今日12大單品的終端零售總價明顯回落，九款產品價格下跌，其中青花郎、國窖1573、習酒君品和古井貢古20的跌幅較突出，下跌端對整體價盤形成明顯拖累。如果12大單品各取一瓶整體打包售賣，今日總售價為10747元，較昨日大跌37元，結束此前高位橫盤，創下近6天以來最低紀錄。

### 2026-10-21 09:02:28  #Trading Economics #Markets #Australia #Currency #Importance 1

Australian Dollar Rallies on RBA Hike Bets — The Australian dollar rose above $0.70, extending its rally to a more than three-week high as hawkish comments from a top central bank official boosted the odds of another interest rate hike. RBA Deputy Governor Andrew Hauser said tighter monetary policy was working, but warned that the inflation outlook remained “materially worse” partly due to elevated oil prices. Hauser stressed the need to anchor inflation expectations ahead of the RBA’s November 3 policy meeting, where policymakers will consider whether to raise rates for a fifth time this year. Markets now price in a 44% chance of a November hike, up from 30% at the start of the week, while the probability of a December move has climbed to 64%. Focus now turns to third-quarter inflation data due next week. Core inflation is expected to rise 1.0% quarter-on-quarter, up from 0.9% previously. Annual core inflation is also projected to remain at 3.5%, well above the RBA’s 2%-3% target range.

【OpenAI稱其智慧體曾削弱提示AI不當行為的系統】OpenAI發現了其AI智慧體削弱內部的異常行為監控系統並關閉警報的事件。近幾個月來，同類事件已發生多起。該公司週二在部落格文章中表示，其正在評估此類案例是否表明其AI模型存在更普遍的逃避監控傾向。OpenAI表示，事件發生在旗下智慧體執行兩項內部程式設計任務期間。根據前述文章，事件發生在OpenAI內部，該公司沒有提及任何受影響的外部方。近幾個月來，對於AI“錯位”(misalignment)的擔憂日益加劇。所謂錯位，就是模型沒有按照建造者的意願執行。此前，OpenAI及Anthropic PBC等同行已披露多起先進AI模型出現此類行為的案例。OpenAI表示，未來將開展更多評估，並執行更嚴格的安全要求，以儘量降低發生此類事件的可能性。不過，OpenAI表示，對於旗下智慧體為何出現上述行為，“尚未找到令人滿意的解釋”。

### 2026-10-21 08:40:11  #公司

【精準打擊違規減持 證監會新規帶來了這些變化】證監會日前釋出《違規轉讓證券案件行政處罰實施規則》，今年12月1日起施行。新規以十八條細則細化了違規減持的認定標準、統一處罰裁量尺度，分類施策、精準定責，向市場清晰地傳遞出了從嚴監管、精準執法的導向。具體看，這份新規最大的亮點，就是告別“模糊裁量”，實現監管的精準化、標準化。以往的案例中，很多時候大家會發現大股東、實控人違規減持的情形非常複雜，在認定的時候就存在彈性空間，裁量尺度上難免有差別，使得市場對於監管預期把握不穩定，震懾違法違規的效果就要打折扣。此次新規將違規轉讓證券行為科學劃分為四大類，區分原始股違規轉讓、突破鎖定期、減持數量與披露不合規、未按要求暫停交易等不同情形；配套差異化的裁量階次，設定“違規轉讓比例+金額”雙指標，從重、一般、從輕梯度清晰，做到“一把尺子量到底”；既避免了處罰上的尺度爭議，又對危害最突出的原始股違規減持設定更高的處罰上限，真正讓違法成本匹配違法危害，也就是剛剛講到的，要體現“從嚴監管、精準執法”的原則導向。（央視）

【韓國出口保持強勁增勢 央行利率決議前夕凸顯經濟韌性】韓國10月出口保持強勁增長勢頭，全球對人工智慧的旺盛需求推動半導體出貨量增長，在央行作出利率決定前夕凸顯了經濟的韌性。據海關週三公佈的資料，經工作日差異調整後，10月前20天的出口同比增長65.9%。9月同期增幅為89.8%。未經調整的資料顯示，出口增長105.4%，進口增長42.8%，貿易順差145億美元。本月前20天出口總額616億美元，9月同期出口額為714億美元。由於今年的中秋節假期在9月，去年在10月，因此同比資料存在偏差，經工作日調整後的資料更能反映潛在趨勢。半導體行業依然是經濟增長的主要引擎，晶片出口額較上年同期飆升222.5%，達到275億美元。石油產品出口增長68.3%，汽車出口增長近41%。這些資料進一步印證了經濟由半導體行業驅動，同時也令通脹保持高位。這些資料還將為韓國央行提供重要考量因素，該行將在週四公佈利率決議。市場普遍預期韓國央行將維持政策不變，因為該行正在權衡近期加息的影響。

【Japan September exports +16.7% YoY; chips and autos strong】Japan's exports rose 16.7% YoY in September, driven by stronger chip and auto shipments, the Finance Ministry said Wednesday, below economists' median forecast of 21.3%. Imports rose 18.9% YoY versus a 20.4% forecast. The non-seasonally-adjusted trade deficit narrowed to ¥536bn in September (about $3.4bn) from a ¥1.1trn shortfall in August. Exports to the US climbed 22.9% YoY and to the EU 10.5%. Crude import value surged 51% YoY as sustained high international oil prices amid Middle East tensions lifted Japan's oil bill.

【中信建投：兆瓦級液冷落地，行業進入標準化放量新階段】中信建投研報稱，兆瓦級液冷落地，行業進入標準化放量新階段。一是產品端，全液冷由晶片級走向整機櫃級，超節點成為新載體，國產廠商推出全球首個兆瓦級單相冷板全液冷超節點，單櫃散熱達1兆瓦、單晶片解熱邁向5000瓦，並實現正交全液冷與全盲插、水電物理隔離。二是行業標準端，首部資料中心冷板式液冷國家標準實施在即，將冷板、分配單元、迴圈泵、管路與快接頭納入統一框架、要求同規格可互換，配套機櫃通用規範與浸沒式介質標準同步出臺，介面不相容的行業痛點被釐清，行業由專案制定制轉入標準化規模交付。三是交付端，訂單能見度逐步明確，頭部公司接到海外客戶單筆製冷裝置訂單摺合逾27億元、交付期壓縮至120天。四是產業趨勢端，需求由晶片擴散至整機櫃全鏈路，其中專用晶片液冷滲透率有望持續上升，並帶來較大增量；國產替代沿核心物料清單由管路、分集水器向快接頭與冷板推進，受驗證週期制約仍屬循序漸進，競爭焦點轉向技術、量產與全球交付能力。中信建投判斷代工鏈利潤先於品牌鏈兌現，四季度至2027年一季度是液冷板塊業績分水嶺，2027年行業將由普遍景氣轉向份額與盈利分化。

### 2026-10-21 08:00:39  #Trading Economics #Economy #Japan #Balance of Trade #Importance 1

Japan Trade Gap Widens Sharply — Japan’s trade deficit widened sharply to JPY 536.6 billion in September 2026 from JPY 277.7 billion in the same month a year earlier, exceeding market expectations of JPY 181.0 billion shortfall and marking a fifth consecutive monthly deficit. The deterioration reflected imports growing faster than exports. Imports rose 18.9% year-on-year to JPY 11,508.8 billion, easing from August’s 28.0% surge and below forecasts of 19.6%, marking the slowest growth since May. Crude oil imports jumped 51.1%, partly reflecting Japan’s efforts to diversify energy procurement amid tensions surrounding the Strait of Hormuz. Meanwhile, exports increased 16.7% to JPY 10,972.2 billion, missing expectations of 20.1% but extending their growth streak to 13 months. Shipments of AI-related semiconductors continued to support exports amid resilient technology demand, although supply-chain disruptions linked to the Middle East conflict posed headwinds.

### 2026-10-21 07:58:53  #Trading Economics #Economy #Japan #Imports YoY #Importance 1

Japan Import Growth Slows to 5-Month Low — Japan’s imports grew by 18.9% year-on-year to JPY 11,508.8 billion in September 2026, falling short of market expectations for a 19.6% gain and slowing from an almost four-year high of 28% rise in August. The latest result marked the softest growth since April. By region, imports rose from China (16.4%), Taiwan (54.9%), South Korea (40.7%), the US (44.8%), ASEAN (16.0%), and Australia (31.1%), while purchases from the EU edged down 0.1% and those from the Middle East fell 7%. By category, mineral fuels led the increase, surging 39.4%, with crude petroleum imports jumping 51.1%, followed by electrical machinery (23.4%), machinery (27.0%), manufactured goods (17.1%), and chemicals (7.1%). Meanwhile, transport equipment imports rose by a more modest 2.2%, while foodstuff purchases increased 6.7%.

【華泰證券：餐廚提油企業有望受益於航空減碳下UCO稀缺增值】華泰證券指出，UCO（廢棄食用油脂）是生物柴油與可持續航空燃料（SAF）的關鍵原料。據測算2024年全球UCO產量約740萬噸（中國佔比約49%），其中68%流向生物燃料行業。中國口徑下UCO包含煎炸老油、泔水油、地溝油等，其中從產生點直接分類收集的烹飪廢油（即國際口徑UCO）須由持證廢棄油脂收運單位回收，頭部餐廚企業憑特許經營權與收運資質構成合規供給的重要來源。華泰證券認為，隨著歐盟UDB全流程追溯與ISCC等認證要求趨嚴，低合規度供給加速出清，合規資質本身即構成稀缺壁壘——具備正規收運資質、全流程可追溯體系與穩定提油能力的頭部餐廚企業有望充分受益於UCO漲價週期，盈利彈性有望凸顯。

Brent Steadies as Traders Weigh US-Iran Talks — Brent crude steadied near $109 per barrel on Wednesday following sharp swings in the previous session, as investors assessed mixed reports on diplomatic progress between the US and Iran. Prices fell sharply on Tuesday after Pakistan’s army chief, Field Marshal Asim Munir, met with senior Iranian diplomatic and military officials in Tehran to discuss bilateral ties and the status of negotiations with Washington. However, a diplomat involved in the discussions dismissed reports that a deal was imminent, while confirming that efforts to end the conflict remain underway. Oil prices have gained more than 10% this month as intensifying hostilities involving Saudi Arabia and Iran-backed Houthi forces, as well as Russia and Ukraine raised supply risks further. At least 18 vessels have also come under attack in the Strait of Hormuz over the past two weeks. Despite these risks, oil flows from the Middle East have largely recovered to around 80% of prewar levels.

【有人鼓吹抵制中國貨？菲前國安顧問反對】近期，中國駐菲律賓大使井泉出席活動，有菲媒體問及其對菲國防部長近期稱中國是本地區“麻煩製造者”這一言論的看法。井泉大使在回應中表示，菲律賓應當感到慶幸，擁有中國這樣一個鄰居，“如果我們要動用武力，完全有可能輕易取勝。但我們不會使用武力，因為我們熱愛和平”。對此，菲律賓部分官員與政客斷章取義、持續抹黑中方，在輿論場上煽動對立，甚至有人鼓吹“抵制中國商品”。這股聲浪並未獲得菲律賓社會廣泛認同，一些菲前政府官員、商界領袖與普通民眾站出來，表示不願看到菲中關係陷入對立。 據“全球菲律賓人雜誌”19日報道，菲律賓前國家安全顧問兼政治學者克拉麗塔·卡洛斯19日在社交媒體上發文，質問那些藉機煽動抵制中國製造商品的菲律賓人。卡洛斯寫道：“抵制中國製造的產品？那麼，你的內褲都來自中國！現在怎麼辦？” 菲律賓商界同樣對菲中關係前景充滿憂慮。同日，菲華商聯總會理事長林榮輝對菲律賓媒體表示，若菲中關係持續惡化，可能會損害菲律賓經濟。他進一步說，“我們對未來非常擔憂”。菲律賓出口商聯合會主席塞爾吉奧·奧蒂斯-路易斯也表示，菲律賓本土製造業和出口業嚴重依賴來自中國的原材料與工業零部件，“我們真的承擔不起徹底推開中國的代價”。 （環球網）

### 2026-10-21 07:27:16  #國際

【俄羅斯柴油重返傳統出口市場】據報道，在美國暫停製裁併推動俄羅斯解除出口禁令後，俄羅斯柴油貨船正重新流向其傳統出口市場。據Kpler和LSEG的船運資料，近日至少有兩艘油輪顯示正駛往土耳其和巴西。俄羅斯於10月9日解除了其自行實施的柴油出口禁令。此前，俄羅斯與美國總統特朗普達成協議向全球市場供應柴油。該協議包括暫時解除美國對俄羅斯燃料供應的制裁，不過歐盟和英國對俄羅斯燃料進口的禁令仍然有效。俄羅斯通常是僅次於美國的全球第二大柴油出口國，但在實施出口禁令之前，夏季其柴油出口量已經減少。資料顯示，今年迄今為止，俄羅斯柴油總出口量約為34.5萬桶/日，較去年下降41%。資料顯示， Leruo號油輪於10月16日從俄羅斯波羅的海港口Primorsk啟航後，目的地顯示為巴西馬瑙斯。另一艘油輪Dolunay號於10月14日從Primorsk裝載柴油離港，目前正駛往土耳其阿拉尼亞。Kpler資料顯示，這兩艘油輪各載有30萬桶柴油。

【馬斯克：SpaceX和特斯拉旗下的Terafab計劃於2028年實現晶片量產】SpaceX與特斯拉共同成立的晶片製造合資企業Terafab，計劃於2028年第三季度實現量產，埃隆·馬斯克週二在SpaceX的電話會議上表示。“我對這個時間表設定了很大的誤差範圍，因為我們尚不確定能否真正實現這一目標，”馬斯克在談及該時間表時表示。該時間表是該專案的粗略計劃，該專案旨在為人工智慧和機器人技術每年提供1太瓦的計算能力，馬斯克本人稱這一時間表“非常激進”。在美國，建設尖端的邏輯晶片工廠通常需要三年或更長時間。 馬斯克表示，在內部規劃中，Terafab的目標是在明年5月或6月左右，於奧斯汀的Terafab研發中心，讓首枚採用靜態隨機存取儲存器 (SRAM) 電路的測試晶片實現正常執行。

1、埃隆・馬斯克回應 SpaceX 與特斯拉持續發酵的合併傳聞，稱兩家公司已設立特別委員會探討合併方案。他表示合併並非確定事項，但認為這是個不錯的選擇。
2、在上季度預測 SpaceX 將於今年年底實現 1000 億美元年度經常性收入後，管理層進一步上調預期，目前預計 2027 年底年度經常性收入可達 5000 億至 1 萬億美元。
3、首席財務官 Bret Johnsen 預測，公司明年第一季度營收將落在 330 億至 370 億美元區間，顯著高於分析師預期。
4、這是 SpaceX 首個盈利季度，淨利潤 44 億美元。SpaceX SI 板塊營收也首次超過星鏈連線業務，證明這項新業務盈利能力強勁、客戶需求旺盛。人工智慧相關投入是 SpaceX 資本開支的主要方向，不過本季度相關支出低於分析師此前預期。
5、星艦下一次發射意義重大，SpaceX 計劃在發射後實現箭體完整回收，這是團隊尚未達成的目標。SpaceX 總裁 Gwynne Shotwell 同時提到，團隊計劃在 2027 年初開展關鍵的星艦在軌加註驗證試驗。

### 2026-10-21 05:18:06  #Trading Economics #Economy #South Korea #Producer Prices Change #Importance 1

South Korea Producer Inflation Eases Slightly — South Korea’s producer prices rose 7.7% year-on-year in September 2026, easing from a 7.9% increase in August. Manufacturing producer prices climbed 12%, driven by a 53.6% surge in coal and petroleum products. Prices for computers, electronic and optical equipment rose 20.5%, while chemical products increased 17.5% and basic metals gained 14.9%. Service prices rose 3.9%, led by a 21.1% increase in financial and insurance service costs. Meanwhile, prices of agricultural, forestry, and marine products advanced 3.3%, while utility prices rose 3.1%. On a monthly basis, the Producer Price Index increased by 0.2% in September.

US Crude Inventories Fall More Than Expected — US crude oil inventories fell by 4.93 million barrels in the week ending October 16th, well above market expectations for a 1.3 million-barrel draw. This followed an 11.12 million-barrel decline in the previous week. Gasoline inventories fell by 2 million barrels, while distillate stocks dropped by 2.6 million barrels. Meanwhile, inventories at Cushing, Oklahoma, kept at the delivery hub for the WTI Crude futures contract, rose by 900,000 barrels, following an increase of 1.823 million barrels in the prior week. US oil production slipped to 13.679 million barrels per day in the week ending October 9th, down from 13.979 million barrels per day a week earlier.

Ibovespa Jumps on Election Trade — The Ibovespa rose 1.6% to close at 208,296 on Tuesday, supported by a new election poll and a positive external backdrop. A Real Time Big Data poll released on Monday showed Flávio Bolsonaro leading President Lula in a presidential runoff, with 52% of valid votes against 48%. Although Bolsonaro has yet to detail his policy plans, investors view his candidacy as more likely to bring fiscal reforms, including spending cuts, which could help lower interest rates. Meanwhile, easing oil prices provided relief to credit-sensitive sectors pressured by energy-driven inflation. Major banks advanced, with Itaú and Bradesco each gaining around 2.9%. Among other financials, B3 jumped 5.4%. Utilities also rose, with Axia up 0.9%, while retailers posted sharp gains, led by Magazine Luiza, which soared 14.8%. In contrast, Vale fell 1.4% as Chinese iron ore prices declined.

Long-term Treasury yields declined alongside energy prices, reinforcing expectations that the Fed will refrain from delivering back-to-back rate hikes at its meeting next week. On the earnings front, 3M (+5.8%) beat profit estimates and raised its full-year outlook for the second consecutive quarter. ### 2026-10-21 03:44:21  #公司 #焦點 #國際

【Anthropic S-1 披露：營收爆發式增長、風險叢生、算力開支天量】據新披露檔案，Anthropic 正為一場超大規模 IPO 做準備，其營收增長迅猛，但面臨一長串風險，包括客戶群狹窄、急需的算力供應存不確定性。
財務情況：IPO草案顯示，2026 上半年營收 163 億美元，去年同期僅 11 億美元；同期淨虧損 807 億美元，遠超 2025 年全年 420 億美元的虧損。其中 720 億是可轉換票據公允價值變動帶來的會計調整，並非現金虧損。算力基礎設施投入持續走高，上半年算力花費近 90 億美元，而 2025 年同期為 23 億美元。公司客戶集中，約兩成營收僅來自兩家客戶。
算力承諾：未來 7–10 年已有鎖定的雲合約約 4500 億美元，疊加晶片租賃義務，已確定的基建支出超 6000 億美元，還在洽談新訂單，承諾總額最高或增至 7500 億。其中將將斥資1110億美元用於谷歌雲算力協議，斥資1100億美元用於AWS雲端計算協議，斥資310億美元用於微軟雲端計算協議。此外還將斥資至多860億美元租用英偉達晶片。公司嘗試自建資料中心，但合作方運營經驗不足。
草案顯示Anthropic已將計劃中的 IPO 推遲至 11 月，有望使其估值達到 2 萬億美元。

Brent Falls on Hopes of US-Iran Diplomacy — Brent fell to $109 a barrel on Tuesday amid hopes of diplomatic progress between the US and Iran. Pakistan’s army chief, Field Marshal Asim Munir, met with senior Iranian diplomatic and military officials in Tehran to discuss bilateral relations and the status of talks with the US. Although officials from both sides have said in recent weeks that messages and proposals are being exchanged through mediators, the extent of their differences on key issues remains unclear. US President Trump said earlier this month that US forces would not attack Iran before the midterm elections. Meanwhile, attacks on tankers and strikes targeting Saudi Arabia have escalated. The US Energy Department also said it was working with US refiners to explore ways to boost fuel output and ease prices. President Trump’s decision to waive key sanctions and tariffs on Russia could ease restrictions on Russian energy trade. However, analysts remain skeptical about the impact of additional Russian diesel supplies on global markets, with Morgan Stanley estimating that Russia’s diesel deal would provide just 12 hours’ worth of supply. Meanwhile, US gasoline inventories showed signs of recovery, with EIA data showing that stockpiles rose by 3.5 million barrels in the week ended October 9th, marking a second consecutive weekly build. ### 2026-10-21 00:17:58  #Trading Economics #Markets #Germany #Stock Market #Importance 0

DAX Closes Marginally Lower — Frankfurt's DAX 40 pared early losses to close marginally down at 25,304 on Tuesday, as weakness in tech and industrials was partly offset by solid gains in utilities and autos. Stabilizing bond yields and the pullback in oil prices offered some relief amid renewed hopes for negotiations to end the war in Iran, following reports of a new proposal from Qatar and Pakistan. Nevertheless, traders remained alert to protests and potential disruptions across Europe, while AI-related stocks faced continued pressure ahead of major US tech earnings. Brenntag led losses among stocks, slipping 3.4%, followed by Zalando (-2.9%), Siemens Energy (-2.1%) and Siemens (-1.8%). Commerzbank and Deutsche Bank fell 1.3% and 0.6%, respectively. On the upside, Qiagen NV surged 5.4%, making it the top performer. E.ON gained 2.9%, on reports that the company is planning a wide-ranging cost-cutting and management overhaul.

European Stocks Inch Lower — European stock indices rebounded from session lows but still closed below the flatline on Tuesday as losses for heavyweight tech and industrial names offset gains in other sectors. The Euro STOXX 50 dropped 0.4% to 6,124 and the STOXX Europe 600 eased 0.1% to 639. ASML fell 2.2%, reigniting its recent volatility after the disappointing IPO of AI-infrastructure developer Elice in Seoul signaled that markets are growing skeptical of the AI sector's increasing need for financing. Likewise, companies with exposure to data center operations declined, with Siemens, Schneider, and Siemens Energy dropping by up to 3.3%. On the other hand, pharmaceutical companies closed sharply higher, with Sanofi adding 3% while Argenx surged 6%.

CAC 40 Falls Amid Tech Losses and Fiscal Crisis — The CAC 40 fell 0.4% to close at 7,838 on Tuesday, extending the previous session’s losses amid renewed selling in global technology stocks. The sector remained under pressure ahead of major tech earnings this week, as concerns over rising AI investment and elevated interest rates weighed on sentiment. Schneider Electric fell 3.3% and STMicroelectronics lost 0.5%. In France, investors also monitored ongoing civil unrest after protests turned violent over the weekend, adding to political and fiscal uncertainty ahead of the April presidential election. Calls for higher public spending are complicating efforts to address social grievances as the country faces mounting pressure to rein in its growing debt burden. Financial shares mostly fell, with BNP Paribas down 0.4%, while most industrial stocks also closed lower. EssilorLuxottica lost 1% amid a sharp slowdown in smart-glasses sales growth.

Italian Stocks Close Flat — Milan’s FTSE MIB closed largely unchanged at 49,071 on Tuesday, as oil prices declined sharply late in the session amid new efforts of de-escalation in the Middle East conflict. Sovereign yields eased, offering some relief to banks, although financials traded mixed amid discussions of a sector contribution to state finances. Intesa’s CEO (-0.4%) criticised the plan, noting that the sector had already committed to a three-year contribution last year. MPS rose 0.3%, while Intesa remained confident in its takeover bid. Falling European gas prices boosted utilities, with Inwit (2.4%) leading gains. Stellantis fell 0.7% while European automakers agreed to an 87.5% emissions reduction target by 2035 with the EU. Luxury remained under pressure, led by Brunello Cucinelli (-1.7%). Tech Technoprobe (-3%) and Prysmian (-2.6%) also declined. Defence Fincantieri fell 0.6%. Energy-related Saipem fell 4.3%, while Eni (-0.7%) reversing initial gains linked to a potential increase in its share buyback.

FTSE 100 Edges Down Slightly — The FTSE 100 closed slightly lower on Tuesday but recovered from session lows as sentiment improved through the afternoon, mirroring a broader reduction in losses across other markets. The rebound coincided with a reversal in oil prices, with Brent crude falling below $108 a barrel after approaching $112 earlier in the session. Gains in healthcare stocks AstraZeneca (+1.5%) and GSK (+0.6%) were outweighed by declines across energy, banking, mining and defence shares. Shell and BP fell, while HSBC and Lloyds Banking Group lost around 1% each. NatWest and Standard Chartered dropped approximately 2%, Rio Tinto and Glencore declined by more than 2%, and Rolls-Royce and BAE Systems slipped 0.7% and 0.5%, respectively. Meanwhile, UK payroll employment rose by 13,000 in September, exceeding expectations for a decline of 8,000. The unemployment rate eased to 4.7% in the three months to August, its lowest since July 2025.

### 2026-10-20 23:40:24  #Trading Economics #Markets #Commodity #Importance 0

Arabica Coffee Futures at Near 2-Month High — Arabica coffee futures traded above $3.10 per pound, holding near their highest since late August, amid concerns over tight inventories and weather-related risks in top producer Brazil. Despite a strong 2026 harvest and ample coffee stocks in Brazil’s producing regions, quality concerns persist, while inventories on international exchanges remain critically low. Certified arabica coffee stocks stood at approximately 240,000 bags in the latest report, their lowest level in nearly 27 years. Operators remained concerned that Brazilian shipments may not reach ICE-certified warehouses before the December contract expires. A firmer Brazilian real has provided additional support by reducing producers’ incentives to sell. Meanwhile, weather conditions across key regions continued to be closely monitored, with a strengthening El Niño towards late 2026 and early 2027 posing risks to rainfall patterns and crop development.

### 2026-10-20 23:33:30  #Trading Economics #Markets #Commodity #Importance 0

European Natural Gas Prices Fall from Peak Levels — European natural gas prices fell more than 3% to €85 per megawatt-hour, retreating from their highest level since December 2022 amid hopes for a diplomatic resolution to the war in Iran. Sentiment improved following an unconfirmed report that Qatar and Pakistan had proposed reviving negotiations to end the conflict. However, supply concerns persist as restricted liquefied natural gas (LNG) shipments from the Gulf complicate Europe’s efforts to replenish storage before winter. European gas inventories reached 74.47% of capacity on October 19, rising steadily in recent weeks but remaining below typical levels for this time of year. Meanwhile, security threats and escalating tensions across the Middle East continue to disrupt energy flows, with regional LNG exports running around 80% below last year’s levels. As colder weather approaches, Europe and Asia, the world’s two largest LNG-importing regions, are likely to compete more intensely for available cargoes.

US Treasury Yields Fall on Tuesday — The yield on the US 10-year Treasury note fell nearly 5 basis points to 5.09% on Tuesday, hovering near one-month lows, as oil prices declined on hopes for a diplomatic resolution to the war in Iran, while weaker housing data added to the downward pressure. Housing starts fell 5.2% in September, while pending home sales dropped to a record low, highlighting the continued impact of elevated mortgage rates on housing demand. Investors also weighed persistent inflationary pressures ahead of the Fed’s interest rate decision next week. The Fed is widely expected to leave rates unchanged, while markets anticipate a 25bps hike in December. Meanwhile, the 2-year Treasury yield, which is particularly sensitive to near-term monetary policy expectations, edged down to 4.76%. The 30-year Treasury yield, which is more sensitive to longer-term inflation expectations and geopolitical risks, also declined to 5.42%.

### 2026-10-20 22:33:10  #Trading Economics #Markets #Commodity #Importance 0

Sugar Futures Remain Above 20 Cents — US sugar futures stayed above 20 cents per pound, close to their highest level since February 2025, amid persistent concerns over tightening supplies and support from elevated oil prices. In top producer Brazil, excessive rainfall linked to El Niño disrupted sugarcane harvesting and crushing operations in the key Center-South region, delaying the harvest and leaving volumes below initial forecasts. Meanwhile, India, the world’s second-largest sugar producer, faces a smaller crop than a year earlier after a powerful El Niño contributed to the weakest monsoon in more than a decade. Insufficient rainfall in key sugarcane-growing regions, particularly Maharashtra and Karnataka, has raised concerns over lower yields. Against this backdrop, the government ordered state-owned fuel retailers to stop procuring ethanol produced from sugar and key sugarcane-based feedstocks from November 1, potentially redirecting more sugarcane supplies towards sugar production.

【美國9月二手房簽約量指數降至紀錄低點 利率高企打壓購房需求】美國9月二手房簽約量降至紀錄低點，高企的按揭貸款利率繼續壓制購房需求。美國全國房地產經紀人協會(NAR)週二公佈的資料顯示，9月二手房簽約量指數大幅下降7.3%，至66，創2001年有紀錄以來最低。該降幅遠超調查的經濟學家預測。自伊朗戰爭爆發以來，按揭貸款利率持續攀升，目前已升至近三年來最高水平。二手房簽約量低迷表明在借貸成本和房價高企之際，房地產市場難以復甦。未經季節因素調整的二手房簽約量指數同比下降近13%。NAR首席經濟學家Lawrence Yun在宣告中表示：“由於按揭貸款利率快速上升，9月二手房市場狀況惡化。買賣雙方可能只是在等待按揭貸款利率朝有利方向變動。”週二公佈的另一份資料顯示，9月新屋開工放緩至2020年以來最低水平之一。

Although Bolsonaro has yet to present detailed policy plans, investors view his candidacy as more likely to bring fiscal reforms, including spending cuts, which are seen as important to lowering interest rates. Major banks traded higher, with Itaú and Bradesco each gaining around 1%. Meanwhile, Petrobras rose nearly 1% as international oil prices remained elevated amid escalating tensions in the Middle East. Elsewhere, utilities were mostly higher, with Sabesp rising nearly 2%. ### 2026-10-20 22:06:40  #Trading Economics #Economy #United States #Pending Home Sales MoM #Importance 2

US Pending Home Sales Fall to Record Low — US pending home sales sank by 7.3% from the previous month in September of 2026 to the lowest level on record, contrasting with market expectations of a 0.5% decrease, according to data compiled by the National Association of Realtors. The pessimistic data was aligned with soaring mortgage rates as the high inflation outlook, widening federal deficits, and resilient economy lifted long-term Treasury yields to decade-highs. Pending sales fell in all areas of the US, including the West (-9.2%), South (-8.2%), Midwest (-6.5%), and the Northeast (-4.2%). “Market conditions weakened in September due to fast-rising mortgage rates,” said NAR Chief Economist Dr. Lawrence Yun. “Although job growth and income gains can support home purchases, housing affordability is more affected by changes in mortgage rates.”

【商務部新聞發言人就美國土安全部發布針對中國企業的聯邦採購排除和移除命令通知答記者問】問：10月19日，美國國土安全部宣佈禁止美聯邦政府民事行政機構採購中國人工智慧企業深度求索及其關聯企業開發的產品和服務，並要求從相關機構的資訊系統中移除相關產品和服務。請問商務部對此有何評論？答：我們注意到有關情況。中方對此強烈不滿、堅決反對。中國政府堅持發展和安全並重，秉持“以人為本、智慧向善”理念，確保人工智慧安全、可控、可靠，推動人工智慧在合規、可信、向善軌道上發展，出臺《網路安全法》《個人資訊保護法》等多部法律法規，依法保護網路安全和資料隱私。中國人工智慧企業一貫致力於為全球使用者提供安全可靠的服務，遵守所在國法律，受到了全球使用者廣泛歡迎。美方罔顧中美元首達成的重要共識，無視中美經貿關係大局，以所謂國家安全為由，對中國人工智慧企業採取歧視性限制措施，是典型的泛化國家安全概念、濫用行政手段打壓中國企業的行為，實質上是將科技經貿問題政治化、工具化，借國家安全之名行打壓遏制之實。這嚴重損害中國企業合法權益，破壞公平競爭的市場環境，干擾中美人工智慧領域正常商業合作。中方敦促美方尊重事實和市場規律，立即糾正錯誤做法，停止對中國企業的無理打壓，撤銷有關歧視性限制措施，為兩國企業正常交流合作創造良好條件。希望美方切實落實兩國元首重要共識，同中方相向而行，用好中美人工智慧對話機制，增進互信、管控分歧，共同推動人工智慧向上向善。中方將密切關注事態進展，對於任何實質性損害中方利益的行為，將採取一切必要措施，堅定維護自身正當合法權益。

【乾旱持續 英國供水公司考慮從挪威進口飲用水】今夏創紀錄的高溫正對英國造成持續影響，英格蘭南部多地10月釋出最高階別乾旱警報。為此，英國東南水務公司表示，如果幹旱持續，將考慮從挪威進口飲用水。英國東南水務公司主要為英格蘭東南部居民供水。該公司水資源部門負責人尼克·普賴斯告訴英國《太陽報》，受今夏持續高溫乾旱影響，目前，位於西薩塞克斯郡的阿丁利水庫蓄水量僅為庫容的19%，只能維持4至6周供應，供水區域內約28萬居民或將在下月面臨斷水風險。為緩解水資源緊張，普賴斯說，公司正考慮從挪威等地以海運方式進口飲用水，同時使用海水淡化裝置將海水轉化為飲用水。不過，普賴斯補充說，從挪威進口飲用水是一項“極端措施”，只會在萬不得已時採用。（新華社）

【美國戰略原油儲備降至44年來最低水平 】美國能源部資料顯示，美國戰略石油儲備計劃中的原油儲量上週降至2.827億桶，為1982年10月以來最低水平。路透社20日援引美國能源部資料包道，美國戰略石油儲備計劃中的原油儲量降至44年來的最低水平。1982年10月15日，美國戰略石油儲備計劃中的原油儲量為2.80802億桶。美國能源部3月11日發表宣告說，將在大約120天內釋放1.72億桶戰略石油儲備，以應對美國和以色列軍事打擊伊朗引發的油價上漲。美國能源部介紹，美國釋放戰略石油儲備可以透過“銷售”和“互換”方式進行。在“互換”方式下，煉油企業因颶風、輸油管道堵塞或航道關閉等緊急情況，可從戰略儲備借出原油，隨後足量歸還。美以伊衝突2月28日爆發前，美國戰略石油儲備計劃中的原油儲量為4.154億桶。（新華社）

【US housing starts fall to four-month low on softer demand】US housing starts slipped in September to a four-month low as demand cooled, with starts down 5.2% YoY to an annualized 1.22 mln, below economists' expectations. Single-family starts fell 4.6% to a one-year low, while multifamily starts dropped nearly 7%. Single-family building permits, a forward indicator of construction activity, fell to their lowest level since early 2023. Affordability pressures and elevated mortgage rates are weighing on the market, and builder confidence has fallen to a 14-year low, suggesting developers may delay new projects until inventories improve.

### 2026-10-20 21:05:51  #央行 #市場

【G30營運長：主要發達經濟體貨幣政策或繼續維持緊縮，進一步加重政府債務償付壓力】10月20日，G30（三十人小組）營運長、曾任國際貨幣基金組織（IMF）戰略、政策與檢查部主任的西拉·帕扎巴西奧格魯（Ceyla PAZARBASIOGLU）在2026外灘年會的圓桌討論中表示，她擔心政府債務償付負擔上升可能加劇財政主導和金融壓抑，並對央行獨立性構成威脅。帕扎巴西奧格魯指出，當前主要發達經濟體面臨長期國債收益率上升、通脹高於目標水平等多重壓力，人工智慧發展和油價變化也為經濟前景增添了複雜性。在此背景下，貨幣政策可能繼續維持緊縮，進一步加重政府債務償付壓力，並引發財政可持續性擔憂。她表示，缺乏財政行動、高債務水平以及中期財政計劃的缺失，都可能推動市場利率進一步上升。“我們正處於一個非常困難的局面。”帕扎巴西奧格魯回顧，20世紀80年代以來，許多經濟體曾經歷更接近“貨幣政策主導”的時期，央行依靠自身政策框架控制通脹，財政當局則在必要時調整財政狀況。但現在，政府債務償付負擔加重，財政調整又難以及時推進。她還表示，發達經濟體發行更多短期債務，增加了政府再融資的不確定性。“這非常令人擔憂，因為它增加了債務滾動風險，並使貨幣政策在財政動態中佔據更加重要的位置。”（澎湃）

【Liu Wei meets IMO Secretary-General Domingos】Oct 20, Shanghai — Minister of Transport Liu Wei met IMO Secretary-General Domingos, who is in China to attend the 2026 North Bund International Shipping Forum. 【Reuters survey: Nearly all economists expect Fed to skip October and hike in December】A Reuters survey found 94 of 100 economists expect the Fed to hold the federal funds rate at 3.75%-4.00% at the October meeting, and all 100 respondents expect a rate increase in December, versus fewer than half who expected a December hike in last month’s poll. Economists noted persistent inflation but said policymakers have signalled patience. Mizuho senior US economist Alex Pelle said the economy is strong and inflation is rising, called skipping October a policy mistake, and forecast the Fed will lift rates in December—likely 25bps but potentially 50bps rather than no hike.

Baltic Dry Index Rises Further to Near 1-Week High — The Baltic Exchange’s dry bulk freight index, which tracks rates for ships carrying dry bulk commodities, was up for a second session on Tuesday, rising about 1.9% to a near one-week high of 2,593 points. The main support came from the bigger-size segment. The capesize index, which typically transports 150,000-ton cargoes, including iron ore and coal, also advanced for a second day, climbing 4.7% to 3,522 points. US Building Permits Higher than Expected — Building Permits in the United States decreased 0.2% month-over-month to 1.400 million units in September 2026, above market expectations of 1.390 million. Single-family permits fell 4% to 845,000, while permits for buildings with five or more units rose 5.9% to 503,000. Regionally, permits fell in the West (-10.2% to 283,000), but rose in the Northeast (13.3% to 145,000), the Midwest (0.5% to 213,000), and the South (1.5% to 759,000).

### 2026-10-20 20:33:43  #Trading Economics #Economy #United States #Housing Starts #Importance 3

US Housing Starts Fall for Third Straight Month — US housing starts fell 5.2% month-on-month to a seasonally adjusted annual rate of 1.221 million in September 2026, marking a third consecutive monthly decline and the lowest level since May’s six-year low of 1.182 million. The reading followed an upwardly revised 1.288 million in August and fell short of market expectations of 1.30 million. The data suggest that elevated mortgage rates continue to weigh on construction activity, prompting builders to adopt a more cautious approach and scale back new-home inventory amid sluggish demand. Single-family housing starts fell 4.6% to 859,000, their lowest level in a year, while multi-family starts dropped 7.2% to a four-month low of 349,000. Regionally, starts declined sharply in the West (-23.2% to 235,000) and Midwest (-19.0% to 158,000), were unchanged in the Northeast at 114,000, and rose 6.1% in the South to 714,000.

### 2026-10-20 20:30:15  #Trading Economics #Economy #Hungary #Interest Rate #Importance 0

Hungary Leaves Rate Unchanged — The National Bank of Hungary left its benchmark interest rate unchanged at 5.5% at its October 2026 meeting, as expected, after four rate cuts this year, the latest being a 25bps reduction in August from 5.75%. Annual inflation remains low, edging up to 1.3% in August from 1.2% in July, below the central bank’s June forecasts. At its September meeting, policymakers lowered the medium-term inflation target to 2.5±1%, to enter the monetary policy horizon from 2028, citing favorable inflation expectations. Average expected inflation was left unchanged for 2026, raised to 3.1% for 2027 and cut to 2.6% for 2028. The economic growth forecasts were lowered to 1.8% for 2026, 2.9% for 2027 and 2.8% for 2028, as extreme drought reduced agricultural output. Policymakers noted that global risks remain elevated, with geopolitical tensions continuing to pressure energy prices.

【柳梧鐵路順利接入國家鐵路網】20日，隨著廣西柳州鐵路樞紐進德站Ⅰ場120號道岔順利插入連線柳州與南寧的柳南客運專線，新建廣西柳州至廣東廣州鐵路柳州至梧州段順利接入國家鐵路網。 國鐵南寧局柳州鐵路工程建設指揮部工程管理部主任馬輝山介紹，柳梧鐵路接入柳南客專施工共需要在進德站插鋪道岔20組，並進行配套的軟硬體改造除錯。站改完成後，柳梧鐵路將全面接入國家鐵路網，為下一步開通運營奠定基礎。 “柳梧鐵路正線鋪軌已基本完成，目前正在有序開展靜態驗收，其中房建專業現場檢查驗收工作已經完成，其他專業也按計劃穩步推進驗收和缺陷整治等工作，計劃11月啟動全線動態檢測。”廣西柳梧鐵路有限公司工程管理部軌道工程師陳志新說。 新建柳州至廣州鐵路是國家中長期鐵路網規劃西南地區至珠三角重要的普速鐵路通道，其中柳梧段正線全長238.167公里，接入益湛鐵路梧州站，是國鐵Ⅰ級雙線電氣化客貨共線鐵路，設計時速160公里。（新華社）

【浙江省統一招投標交易平臺啟用】20日下午，浙江省統一招投標交易平臺啟用儀式在杭州舉行。省委書記、省人大常委會主任王浩宣佈省統一招投標交易平臺啟用。劉捷指出，省委、省政府把深入推進招投標領域治理改革，列為牽一髮動全身的重大改革事項。全省統一、上下貫通的招投標交易平臺建成啟用，開啟了改革向更深領域拓展、向更高水平躍升的新階段。各地各部門要錨定目標任務，持續聚力攻堅，建好用好這一平臺，引領打造規範有序的招投標市場，營造一流招投標領域營商環境。要不斷迭代最佳化功能、豐富拓展場景、守牢安全底線，推動平臺更加好用易用；做好新舊系統平穩過渡銜接，加快全域貫通，推動交易資料全量納入，進一步護航重大專案全生命週期管理，提升平臺實戰實效水平；加強招投標領域突出問題專項整治，深化以案促改、以案促治，強化穿透式常態化監管，努力將平臺優勢轉化為治理效能。（浙江釋出）

⑦通用汽車股價延續跌勢，盤前下跌6.3%。
⑧明晟公司股價盤前上漲4.7%，此前公佈的第三季度業績超出預期。

### 2026-10-20 19:50:22  #Trading Economics #Markets #United States #Stock Market #Importance 2

US Futures Waver — Futures tracking US stocks were mostly lower on Tuesday as the tech sector remained volatile on fresh worries about data center financing ahead of key earnings results. Contracts for the S&P 500 were slightly down, while those for the Nasdaq 100 fell 0.6% and the Dow was flat. South Korean AI-infrastructure provider Elice plunged 32% following its IPO in Seoul, the latest in a series of signals that markets have grown fatigued by the soaring levels of financing that chipmakers and data center operators are asking for. CoreWeave sank 5% premarket following the plunge in its bonds last week. Micron, Marvell, and Arm were nearly 3% lower. Meanwhile, SpaceX dropped 1% ahead of its recently announced earnings results due after the closing bell, which may have been brought forward to enable another capital raise. On the other hand, traditional economic sectors consolidated following losses yesterday. Walmart, ExxonMobil, and Eli Lilly gained over 1%.

【山東加力推進美麗中國省域先行區建設】10月20日，記者從山東省人民政府新聞辦公室舉行的省政府政策例行吹風會上瞭解到，近年來山東在產業綠色化、能源低碳化方面取得明顯成果，有力推動美麗中國省域先行區建設。綠色低碳轉型是美麗中國省域先行區建設的關鍵。山東省發展改革委副主任胡芳營介紹，山東精準管控“兩高”、壓減煤炭消費，“十四五”期間全部能源消費中煤炭佔比已從61.1%下降至49.6%、下降了11.5個百分點，萬元GDP能耗強度下降21.4%、高於國家激勵目標5.9個百分點；重點行業先進產能佔比超過40%，“十四五”期間全省高新技術產業產值佔比由45.1%提升到55.7%，六大高耗能行業增加值佔比從42%下降至39.7%。此外，山東綠色轉型加力提速。目前全省30萬千瓦及以下發電抽凝機組全部替代退出，膠東半島核電、海上風電等大型清潔能源基地加快建設，去年一年發出2245.4億度綠電、相當於減少二氧化碳排放1.8億噸；截至今年9月底，非化石能源裝機佔比已達55.9%、比2020年提高25個百分點。（新華社）

### 2026-10-20 18:24:33  #Trading Economics #Economy #Portugal #Producer Prices Change #Importance 1

Portugal Producer Inflation Highest Since 2023 — Portugal’s industrial producer inflation rose to 9.2% year-on-year in September 2026, the highest since February 2023, marking the third consecutive month of acceleration and up from a downwardly revised 7.1% in August. The increase was almost entirely driven by energy, with inflation surging to 33.6% from 22.8% in the previous month, as prices of petroleum products continued to climb sharply and, to a lesser extent, electricity prices. Inflation also picked up for intermediate goods (8.2% vs 7.5%), consumer goods (1.9% vs 1.7%) and capital goods (1.5% vs 1.4%). By sector, inflation rose most sharply for utilities (20.9% vs 12.8%) and manufacturing (7.3% vs 5.8%), while easing for mining (69.3% vs 72.5%), although remaining elevated. On a monthly basis, producer prices rose 1.5%, accelerating from a downwardly revised 0.7% increase in August, driven solely by a 7.9% rise in energy prices.

### 2026-10-20 18:18:26  #Trading Economics #Markets #Commodity #Importance 0

Brent Crude Nears War-Driven Highs — Brent crude rose above $111 a barrel on Tuesday, reaching its highest level in five months and moving to within $3 of the peak recorded in early May, shortly after the conflict in the Middle East began. Prices climbed as renewed Houthi attacks and reports of vessel strikes in the Gulf heightened concerns over regional supply security. Yemen’s Iran-aligned Houthi movement claimed attacks on Saudi energy, airport and military facilities, including an Aramco site in Yanbu and the Shaybah oilfield. Shipping tracker Kpler reported that at least 18 vessels had reportedly been attacked over the previous two weeks, adding to concerns about disruptions to maritime trade and supporting the geopolitical risk premium in oil prices. Analysts cautioned that the risk premium could ease if oil shipments continue uninterrupted, although there are currently few signs of such a development. Meanwhile, US crude and refined-product inventories were expected to have declined last week.

Luxembourg Records Highest Jobless Rate in 6 Years — Luxembourg’s seasonally adjusted unemployment rate climbed to 6.5% in September 2026, its highest level since July 2020, compared with 6.4% in August and 6.1% a year earlier. The number of unemployed rose by 82 to 20,889, with the increase particularly pronounced among highly qualified job seekers at 10.6% year-on-year, although slowing from the 19% increase recorded in August. The most affected age group were those aged between 16 and 29, with unemployment rising by 10.7%, while those aged 45 to 64 were significantly less affected (7.6%). Total employment also rose by 130 to 322,936, while the labot force population rose by 212 to 323,825. Regarding cross-border employment, incoming cross-border workers rose by 513 to 236,204, while the number of outgoing resident workers rose by 29 to 15,581.

Bulgaria Current Account Swings to Deficit in August — Bulgaria’s current account shifted to a deficit of EUR 96.0 million in August 2026 from a surplus of EUR 245.6 million in the same month a year earlier. The deterioration was driven by a wider goods deficit of EUR 535.3 million, compared with EUR 463.4 million, alongside a narrower services surplus of EUR 970.5 million versus EUR 1,107.7 million, reflecting weaker manufacturing services (nil vs EUR 18.0 million) and travel (EUR 718.0 million vs EUR 737.4 million). As a result, the combined goods and services surplus narrowed to EUR 435.2 million from EUR 644.3 million. Meanwhile, the primary income deficit widened to EUR 688.6 million from EUR 464.2 million, while the secondary income surplus increased to EUR 157.4 million from EUR 65.5 million. In January-August 2026, the current account recorded a deficit of EUR 5,552.4 million, compared with a deficit of EUR 2,225.1 million a year earlier.

The unemployment rate unexpectedly fell to 4.7% in the three months to August, its lowest level since July 2025, while regular wage growth accelerated to 3.7%, slightly above forecasts of 3.6%. The data followed a series of stronger-than-expected releases last week, with the economy expanding 0.1% in August and the trade deficit narrowing unexpectedly, suggesting greater economic resilience than previously anticipated. Meanwhile, some major investors expect fiscal discipline to feature prominently in next week’s budget, helping support demand for gilts. Since Andy Burnham became prime minister, UK gilt yields have risen less than those in several other major bond markets, reflecting comparatively stronger investor confidence in the UK’s fiscal outlook.

Spain Trade Gap Widens in August — Spain’s trade shortfall rose to €7.81 billion in August 2026 from €5.98 billion in the same period a year ago. Imports jumped by 16.7% yoy to €35.4 billion, boosted by a 41.9% jump in purchases of energy products, of which oil & derivatives. Solid increases were also seen for raw materials (+38.3%); automotive (+14.7%); chemical products (+14.3%), particularly medicines (+18.5%) and essential oils & perfumes (+16.2%); and consumer manufactures (+12.1%), especially apparel (+17.3%). Among key partners, imports rose from the EU (+13.5%), particularly France, Italy and Portugal; the US (+24.2%) and China (+18.9%). Meanwhile, exports surged by 13.3% to €27.6 billion, mainly on account of energy products (+79.3%), namely oil & derivatives and coal and electricity. Shipments also grew markedly for raw materials (+42.7%); chemical products (+14.6%) and consumer manufactures (+11.8%). Exports rose mostly to the EU (+13.3%), led by France and Portugal; the US (+50.7%) and China (+41.5%).

Eurozone Construction Output Expands at Fastest Pace in 10 Months — Eurozone construction output rose by 1.4% year-on-year in August 2026, marking its strongest growth since October 2025 and rebounding from an upwardly revised 1.7% decline in July. Specialised construction activities increased by 3.1%, rebounding following a 1.2% decline, while the pace of decline eased in the construction of buildings to 3.7% from 6.5%. Meanwhile, civil engineering remained flat after edging up 0.2% in the prior month. Construction activity rose most in Germany, rising sharply by 10.5% following a 1.1% increase. In contrast, output continued to decline in Spain (-4.3% vs. -10.6%), Italy (-2.2% vs. -0.1%) and France (-7.8% vs. -5.0%). On a monthly basis, eurozone construction output rose by 1.8%, accelerating from 0.2% in July. Across the EU, output increased by 2.6%, recovering from a 1.6% decline in the previous month.

### 2026-10-20 17:11:06  #Trading Economics #Economy #Germany #ZEW Economic Sentiment Index #Importance 3

German Investor Sentiment Weakens in October — Germany’s ZEW Indicator of Economic Sentiment fell to 28.1 in October, down from September’s seven-month high of 34.7 and below market expectations of 30, reflecting renewed concerns over the strength of the recovery. ZEW President Achim Wambach noted that the economy is gradually emerging from stagnation, but the recovery remains fragile as high energy prices weigh on household purchasing power, raise production costs and keep inflation elevated. By sectors, banks and insurance companies recorded sharp declines of 18.4 and 9.0 points, respectively, although their readings remained firmly positive. Sentiment also deteriorated in services, falling 6.8 points, and telecommunications, down 8.2 points, while the outlook for private consumption weakened. In contrast, steel and metal production and electrical engineering provided positive momentum. Meanwhile, the assessment of current economic conditions improved significantly, rising 11.2 points to -35.9, its highest level since May 2023.

【China ministry urges AI-enabled breeding to accelerate breakthrough seed varieties】China's Ministry of Agriculture and Rural Affairs, at a meeting in Guangzhou, ordered a stepped-up push to accelerate AI-enabled breeding and fast-track development of breakthrough seed varieties to meet seed-industry 15th Five-Year targets and cultivate internationally competitive seed-technology leaders. It called for stronger germplasm protection and faster identification, improvement and shared use of genetic resources to raise protection and utilization efficiency. The ministry directed organized, systematized R&D and a one-variety-one-policy approach to shore up weaknesses. It ordered modernization of national seed bases, wider adoption of advanced equipment and measures to improve seed-supply quality. The ministry required full-chain variety management, large-scale demonstration and promotion of superior varieties, tighter intellectual-property protection and strict enforcement against counterfeiting and infringement. Local governments and agencies were instructed to prioritize, coordinate and accelerate projects to deliver breakthrough progress in seed-industry revitalization.

【SAFE: Sept 2026 non-bank cross-border net inflow $40.7bn, down 35% MoM】State Administration of Foreign Exchange deputy head and spokesperson Li Bin said non-bank cross-border net inflows from corporates and individuals were $40.7bn in September 2026, a 35% MoM decline. Cross-border receipts and FX settlement volumes rose 9% and 17% MoM respectively; market expectations were broadly stable and corporate settlement and sales rates both edged up. Goods trade continued to deliver large net inflows; the services deficit was stable, with strong outbound and inbound travel keeping travel receipts and payments elevated. Outward direct investment increased, and onshore financing to overseas affiliates rose rapidly—linked to domestic and external interest-rate moves and, to some extent, reflecting a stronger RMB financing currency function.

【國家外匯管理局副局長、新聞發言人李斌就2026年9月外匯市場形勢答記者問】日前，國家外匯管理局公佈了2026年9月銀行結售匯和銀行代客涉外收付款資料。國家外匯管理局副局長、新聞發言人李斌就2026年9月外匯市場形勢回答了記者提問。問：9月以來外部市場環境變化較大，請問我國外匯市場執行態勢如何？答：9月以來，歐美日央行先後加息，部分經濟體匯市、債市出現較大波動，國際金融市場繼續震盪調整。我國外匯市場執行總體穩健。從整體情況看，涉外交易保持活躍，9月我國跨境收支和結售匯規模環比分別增長9%和17%。市場預期基本平穩，企業等主體結匯率和售匯率環比均有所上升。跨境資金保持淨流入，9月企業、個人等非銀行部門跨境資金淨流入407億美元，環比下降35%。從渠道特點看，貨物貿易項下資金延續較大規模淨流入。服務貿易逆差相對穩定，其中，我國居民出境旅行、外籍人員來華旅行意願均較強，旅行收入和支出保持較高水平。企業對外直接投資增多，其中境內向境外關聯企業提供融資較快上升，與內外部利率變化有關，一定程度上也反映出人民幣融資貨幣功能增強。

### 2026-10-20 16:38:07  #MKT News #Market Regions #Greater China

【China drafts ban on L2 'small blue' autonomous-driving indicator lights】On Oct. 20 the Ministry of Industry and Information Technology’s Equipment Industry Division I published a draft Installation Requirements for External Lighting and Optical Signal Devices of Motor Vehicles and Trailers (for comment). It bars vehicles without Level‑3 or higher automated driving systems from installing ADS indicator lights. The draft mandates ADS lights use a blue‑green color (previous L2 assist lights were commonly blue). When a Level‑3 or higher automated driving function is activated, all ADS lights must automatically illuminate and remain lit while the system is active; ADS lights are prohibited from illuminating at Level‑2 or below and must automatically extinguish when the automated driving system disengages. Manual on/off control of ADS lights is prohibited.

### 2026-10-20 16:36:32  #其他

【“十五五”時期我國將加力攻關提升種業創新水平】農業現代化，種子是基礎。農業農村部有關負責人19日表示，“十五五”時期將在有組織體系化育種攻關上再加力，加快提升種業創新水平。這是記者在農業農村部19日於廣東省廣州市召開的深入實施種業振興行動推進會上瞭解到的。2021年7月，中央深改委第二十次會議審議透過《種業振興行動方案》。經過持續攻堅，我國培育出抗赤黴病小麥、抗稻飛蝨水稻、耐密宜機收玉米、高油高產大豆等一批生產急需的重大品種，更好保障國家糧食安全、更好守護人們的美好生活。農業農村部有關負責人表示，“十五五”時期種業振興面臨新形勢新要求，任務仍然艱鉅。如糧食和重要農產品需求發生深刻變化，必須把準種業發力方向，育種上既要注重產量，也要注重品質；氣候變化對農業生產帶來嚴峻挑戰，必須把抗逆穩產擺在更加突出位置等。未來5年，是種業振興“十年實現重大突破”的攻堅階段。“我國育種創新已取得多點突破，但從0到1的原創性成果還不多，高油高產大豆、耐密宜機收玉米等品種的單產水平、抗逆性狀，以及奶牛、肉牛、生豬等畜種的飼料轉化率、繁殖效率等效能與國外先進水平仍有差距。”農業農村部有關負責人說，要統籌實施國家種業科技專案和育種聯合攻關，圍繞大面積單產提升、標準化設施種養殖等需求，加快培育一批突破性品種。還要加大育種創新支援力度，推動人工智慧賦能育種創新，加力打造一流種業企業。（新華社）

### 2026-10-20 16:26:10  #宏觀 #市場

【工信部：任何汽車研發活動都要以安全為第一前提】10月20日，國務院新聞辦公室舉行新聞釋出會介紹2026年前三季度工業和資訊化發展情況。工業和資訊化部總工程師王衛明表示，近年來，在各方面的團結協作積極進取下，我國汽車產業技術創新活躍，產品迭代更新速度快，企業生產製造能力和質量管理水平不斷提高，中國品牌汽車功能和效能不斷提升。但是也出現部分企業跟風推出新產品、驗證不充分的問題。為了進一步提升汽車產品安全技術效能，保障公共安全和公民生命財產安全，近日，工業和資訊化部會同公安部、生態環境部、市場監管總局等部門研究編制了《關於進一步加強汽車產品創新設計和研發測試驗證有關管理工作的通知》（下稱《通知》），重點包括三個方面。一是新增相關技術引數，壓實企業責任。《通知》明確要求企業填報隱藏式門把手、電吸門等創新設計和環境適應性的研發測試，驗證有關技術引數，並將其納入工信部汽車產品准入和生產一致性管理，對於存在未按要求申報、設施驗證不充分等問題的汽車產品不予准入，守牢產品安全關口。二是加強汽車產品創新設計安全評估，針對異形方向盤、虛擬操縱件等20多項汽車產品創新設計，《通知》前置安全風險評估等事項總體要求和21項具體要求，目的是督促指導企業對車輛功能安全性、駕駛安全性、操控便利性、維修經濟性等進行充分評估驗證，其中要求電吸門必須具備防夾功能，禁止在車輛行駛狀態下使用零重力座椅、旋轉座椅、升頂座艙等功能，更好地保障消費者用車安全。三是充分開展汽車產品研發測試驗證，同時提出建立健全產品研發測試驗證管理制度等6項總體要求、8項具體要求，將22項推薦性標準、行業標準上升為強制性要求，督促指導企業開展綜合可靠性與耐久性等7方面的測試驗證，其中可靠性測試驗證不低於12萬公里，環境適應性測試驗證週期不少於一年，要求汽車生產企業應進行動力蓄電池迴圈效率、耐久性等實測驗證，不得直接採信供應商的認證報告。安全是汽車企業的生命線與核心競爭力，任何研發活動都要以安全為第一前提，絕不能以犧牲安全為代價。（證券時報）

### 2026-10-20 16:20:48  #Trading Economics #Economy #Taiwan #New Orders #Importance 1

Taiwan Export Orders Hit New Record High — Taiwan’s export orders surged 62.4% year-on-year to a record USD 114 billion in September 2026, although easing from 71.4% in the previous month. Growth in export orders moderated for information and communication products (87.3% vs 100.5% in August), electronic products (71% vs 83.9%), machinery (21.5% vs 28.3%), electrical machinery products (7.1% vs 14.7%), and mineral products (73% vs 91.6%). In contrast, growth accelerated for basic metals and articles thereof (29.7% vs 26.8%), while orders for transport equipment rebounded sharply (5.8% vs -0.1%). Despite the moderation across several categories, export orders exceeded USD 100 billion for a second consecutive month, underscoring the strength of AI-driven demand for Taiwan, a leading global supplier of AI chips and related technology. Among major markets, export order growth remained robust, led by the US (79.4%), Europe (68%), Mainland China and Hong Kong (48.5%), ASEAN countries (41.1%), and Japan (35.5%).

### 2026-10-20 16:15:38  #市場 #國際

【法國市場遭遇股債匯三殺】近段時間，法國多地民眾持續抗議生活成本高漲，部分地區抗議演變為騷亂。法國這次社會動盪，對資本市場帶來了怎樣直接影響？對法國以及歐洲經濟又將有何深層次衝擊？ 週一，法國股市、債市和匯市全線走弱。其中，國債承壓尤為明顯，法國10年期國債收益率上漲13個基點至4.9%。其與德國同期限國債的利差也擴大至146個基點，為兩週內最高水平。除了收益率和利差走高，法國債市面臨的另一項潛在風險，還在於海外投資者持倉比例偏高。有資料顯示，外資持有法國政府債務的比例約為50%，這高於義大利的28%、美國的30%。在一般情況下，廣泛的國際投資者基礎有助於政府融資，但是當風險偏好下降、政治不確定性增加時，海外投資者也可能迅速調整資產配置，從而放大債券市場的價格波動。有分析人士表示，如果要在其他調整空間有限的情況下填補財政缺口，那麼企業就可能率先成為被“開刀”的物件。另外，由於法國債券市場規模龐大，並且深度嵌入歐洲的金融體系，因此法國債市的波動極易向其他國家傳導。 （央視財經）

### 2026-10-20 16:10:38  #Trading Economics #Economy #Italy #Current Account #Importance 1

Italy Current Account Surplus Widens in August — Italy’s current account surplus widened to EUR 2,766 million in August 2026 from EUR 2,312 million in the corresponding month a year earlier. The goods surplus increased to EUR 2,280 million from EUR 2,139 million in August 2025, while the primary income surplus rose to EUR 1,798 million from EUR 1,308 million. Meanwhile, the services surplus decreased to EUR 633 million from EUR 735 million, while the secondary income deficit widened to EUR 1,946 million from EUR 1,869 million. The increase in the overall surplus reflected a stronger balance in goods and primary income, despite the wider secondary income deficit.

### 2026-10-20 16:10:37  #Trading Economics #Economy #Euro Area #Current Account #Importance 1

Eurozone Current Account Surplus Narrows Sharply in August — The eurozone current account surplus fell sharply to €7.7 billion in August 2026, down from €18.1 billion a year earlier. The goods surplus declined to €10.5 billion, its lowest level since April 2023, while the primary income account swung to a €2.3 billion deficit from a €2.6 billion surplus. The secondary income deficit also widened to €17.0 billion from €15.1 billion. Meanwhile, the services surplus increased to €16.5 billion from €14.6 billion in August 2025. For the first eight months of 2026, the cumulative current account surplus narrowed to €130.2 billion, down from €148.2 billion over the same period last year.

【深交所開展“上市公司全球行——阿聯酋專場”系列活動】
近日，深交所聯合阿聯酋投資部等合作方，在深圳開展“上市公司全球行——阿聯酋專場”系列活動，組織9家人工智慧算力基礎設施、智慧消費電子等領域深市上市公司，與阿聯酋政府部門、企業及投資機構開展“一對一”對接、走進上市公司等形式交流，深入挖掘跨境投融資及產業合作機遇。
本次活動在充分摸排阿方產業需求、投資偏好和合作意向的基礎上，立足深市產業特色匹配對接主體，有利於促進上市公司投資價值、技術優勢、產業資源與阿方精準對接，更好支援兩國企業間產業和投融資合作。近年來，深交所持續深化與中東資本市場務實合作，與當地政府部門搭建需求對接機制，與交易所簽署合作諒解備忘錄，與主權基金等長期投資者建立常態化交流渠道，在境內外聯合舉辦市場宣介和專題培訓等活動，逐步形成市場培育、投融資對接、產品開發的多層次合作模式。
下一步，深交所將在中國證監會領導下，持續推進資本市場高水平對外開放，以2026年APEC會議在深圳舉辦為契機，依託“上市公司全球行”品牌活動，進一步提升跨境服務的針對性和實效性，更好對外宣介中國資本市場和深市上市公司，助力上市公司用好“兩個市場、兩種資源”，實現高質量發展。（深交所釋出）

### 2026-10-20 16:05:26  #Trading Economics #Economy #Italy #Construction Output #Importance 0

Italy Construction Output Falls Most Since 2024 — Italy’s construction output fell 2.2% year-on-year on a calendar-adjusted basis in August 2026, following a revised 0.1% decline in the previous month and marking the sharpest contraction since December 2024. On a seasonally adjusted monthly basis, construction output decreased by 2.9%, extending the decline to three consecutive months and marking the steepest drop since December 2024. On average, construction production fell 3.2% in the June-to-August period from the previous three-month period. In the first eight months of 2026, calendar-adjusted construction output rose 1.2%, while the unadjusted index increased 2.3%.

【工信部：分層分類推進國家級先進製造業叢集提質能級躍升】10月20日，國務院新聞辦公室舉行新聞釋出會介紹2026年前三季度工業和資訊化發展情況。工業和資訊化部總工程師王衛明表示，一艘船、一架飛機、一輛汽車整車背後不是單一企業的單打獨鬥，而是一條產業鏈的高效協同，一個創新網路的緊密聯動，一套產業生態的合力支撐。下一步，工業和資訊化部將深入貫徹全國先進製造業大會精神，系統性地推進國家級叢集提質升級，能級躍升。首先突出分層、分類施策，對已經具備較強國際競爭優勢的叢集，重點提升創新資源、創新策源能力、全球資源配置能力，加快向世界級、世界級叢集邁進。對於處於加速成長期的叢集，聚焦補短板、鍛長板、強主體、優生態，把產業發展潛力實打實地轉化為核心競爭力；其次，深化跨域協同聯動，引導先進製造業叢集深化跨區域資源共享，跨領域技術融合，產業鏈上下游融通發展，打破地域壁壘和行業邊界，形成“握指成拳”的合力。最後，強化政策精準支援，從創新平臺佈局，高階人才引育，要素資源保障等方面，加大對先進製造業叢集的支援力度，推動叢集從物力集聚向核心能力系統整合躍升，為構建以先進製造業為骨幹的現代化產業體系提供硬核支撐。

### 2026-10-20 16:03:16  #Trading Economics #Markets #Germany #Government Bond 10Y #Importance 1

European Bond Yields Ease but Risks Persist — European government bond yields edged lower after Monday’s sharp rise, though borrowing costs in heavily indebted euro-area countries remained near multi-year highs amid social unrest and fiscal concerns. Germany’s 10-year Bund yield held at 3.44%, supported by safe-haven demand, while France’s eased to 4.9% and Italy’s stood at 4.57%. The 10-year OAT-Bund spread remained around 147 basis points, its widest level since the eurozone sovereign debt crisis. In France, curfews were imposed in several cities amid anti-government protests, potentially complicating austerity efforts ahead of the 2027 elections. Italy also announced plans to deploy 500 troops following days of unrest. Meanwhile, geopolitical tensions intensified after Ukrainian President Volodymyr Zelenskyy accused US President Donald Trump of pressuring Kyiv to make concessions to Russia, while persistent inflation concerns and expectations of higher-for-longer interest rates continued to weigh on sentiment.

【JPMorgan cuts CATL H-share to Neutral; keeps A-share Overweight】JPMorgan cut its rating on CATL H-shares (03750.HK) to Neutral, kept an Overweight on A-shares and lowered its earnings estimates. The bank said it is increasingly concerned industry profitability is deteriorating faster than expected as profits shift across the EV supply chain. Policy changes — a battery consumption tax, lower export rebate rates — and rising trade barriers are pressuring automaker margins. JPMorgan warned CATL may be hit first but could ultimately emerge a net beneficiary if cost pass-through or absorption increases customer stickiness.

【小摩：降寧德時代H股評級至“中性” 整車廠盈利能力已承壓】摩根大通釋出研報稱，將寧德時代H股評級下調至“中性”，A股維持“增持”評級，並下調寧德時代盈利預測，同時維持寧德時代A股為該行所覆蓋電池股中唯一評級“增持”的股份。寧德時代將於今日(20日)收市後公佈第三季業績，該股今日股價下挫，盤中創去年8月底以來逾一年新低。摩根大通稱，日益擔心行業盈利能力惡化的速度快於預期，電動汽車產業鏈的利潤正經歷更廣泛的重新分配。電池消費稅、出口退稅率下調及不斷增加的貿易壁壘接踵而至，整車廠盈利能力已經承壓。該行指出，寧德時代可能最先受到衝擊，但最終或成為較大贏家，因無論其將成本壓力轉嫁，還是自行消化部分負擔，最終結果都可能是客戶黏性增強等。該行稱，寧德時代H股較在岸A股存在溢價，料反映在岸投資者對政策不確定性持更審慎立場，而全球基金似乎已押注長期潛在增長強勁。

### 2026-10-20 15:55:03  #Trading Economics #Markets #Commodity #Importance 1

Aluminum Remains Near Multi-Month Lows — Aluminum futures in the UK hovered above $3,000 per ton, remaining near multi-month lows amid improving supply outside the Middle East. China continues to offset reduced supply from the Gulf, with domestic production running close to record levels. Chinese aluminum exports rose 25.9% year-on-year in September, reaching their highest monthly level in three months, while Indonesian exports exceeded 150,000 tonnes in August. Although production across the Gulf Cooperation Council remains depressed, some smelters have begun to recover gradually. Emirates Global Aluminium said in late August that a quarter of the reduction cells at its Al Taweelah plant had been brought back online, with a full recovery in production expected by the first quarter of 2027. Adding to the positive supply outlook, Rio Tinto reached an agreement to extend operations at its Bell Bay Aluminium smelter in Tasmania through 2031.

### 2026-10-20 15:53:04  #央行 #市場

【5年期以上LPR報3.5% 專家：短期內LPR定價基礎會保持穩定】10月20日，中國人民銀行授權全國銀行間同業拆借中心公佈，最新貸款市場報價利率（LPR）為：1年期LPR為3.0%，5年期以上LPR為3.5%。以上LPR在下一次釋出LPR之前有效。東方金誠研究團隊王青、馮琳指出，10月兩個期限品種的LPR報價保持不變，符合市場預期。主要是9月末央行調整完善若干貨幣政策工具，主要政策利率保持穩定，這意味著10月LPR報價的定價基礎沒有變化。另外，當前銀行淨息差仍然處在歷史低位附近，報價行也缺乏主動下調LPR報價加點的動力。王青、馮琳進一步認為，近期LPR報價保持穩定，根本原因是三季度GDP（國內生產總值）增速回升到4.5%，以高技術製造業為代表的新質生產力領域加速發展。在此背景下，總量型貨幣政策保持定力，貨幣政策加大逆週期調節力度，優先選擇結構性貨幣政策工具發力。（每經）

### 2026-10-20 15:52:06  #MKT News #XAUUSD #Impact bullish

【World Gold Council: digital-gold market $45–55bln; baseline forecast $160–170bln by 2031】World Gold Council report estimates the digital-gold market at $45–55bln. Digital gold converts verified physical gold into tradable digital standard units to support tokenization, custodial products and collateral, without constraints of bar form or storage location. A five-year baseline scenario projects roughly 27% annualised growth to $160–170bln by 2031. The physical gold investment market is about $13tln; digital forms (vault-based digital gold and tokenized gold) currently represent under 1% of that market. Tokenized-gold cumulative spot trading volume in Q1 2026 reached $90bln, exceeding full-year 2025 volume.

### 2026-10-20 15:49:48  #Trading Economics #Economy #Poland #Industrial Production #Importance 1

Poland Industrial Output Rises More Than Expected — Poland’s industrial production rose by 4.6% year-on-year in September 2026 from a downwardly revised 4.0% increase in the previous month, surpassing market expectations of 3.1%. Output growth accelerated in manufacturing (4.0% vs 3.1% in August), driven largely by gains in beverages (9.1% vs 6.1%), printing and reproduction of recorded media (1.0% vs 0.3%), chemicals and chemical products (8.7% vs 0.9%), and computer, electronic, and optical products (12.6% vs 7.1%). Moreover, production increased in electricity, gas, steam, and air-conditioning supply (10.2% vs 9.1%). On the other hand, output moderated in mining and quarrying (12.9% vs 18.2%) and water supply, sewerage, waste management, and remediation activities (8.2% vs 9.6%). On a monthly basis, industrial production jumped by 17.0% in September, rebounding sharply from a 7.8% drop in August. In the January–September period, industrial output was 3.6% higher than in the same period last year.

French Bond Yields Ease After Sharp Rise — France’s 10Y OAT yield edged down to 4.9% after rising 15 basis points on Monday, marking its largest one-day increase since mid-July, as social unrest and concerns over the government’s ability to rein in the budget deficit continued to weigh on sentiment. Authorities imposed curfews in several cities on Monday to contain nationwide anti-government protests, adding to pressure on President Emmanuel Macron and potentially complicating efforts to implement austerity measures ahead of the 2027 elections. The National Assembly is set to vote on the revenue section of the 2027 draft budget on Tuesday, though approval would not guarantee passage of the full bill. Prime Minister Sébastien Lecornu may still need to invoke Article 49.3 of the Constitution to push the budget through without a parliamentary vote by December 17. French government bonds have also come under pressure from a broader global bond selloff, driven by renewed inflation concerns and expectations of higher interest rates.

著力激發行業增長活力，加強對非理性競爭的綜合治理，落實好首臺（套）應用政策，持續完善產業生態。舉辦高質量品牌活動，廣泛凝聚發展共識。（央視新聞）

一是規模“穩”，裝備工業“壓艙石”作用持續鞏固。前三季度裝備工業增加值同比增長7.1%，對工業增長貢獻率達26.6%。高階裝備供給能力增強，工業機器人、數控機床、新能源汽車產量分別同比增長29.1%、13.5%、11%。全國造船完工量同比增長50.7%。

二是產品“新”，創新產品亮點紛呈。首列具身智慧數字地鐵列車9月在柏林國際軌道交通展亮相，城市軌道交通裝備取得新進展；智慧機器人化身店員製作咖啡、售賣商品，人工智慧加速落地民生場景；國產第二艘大型郵輪“愛達·花城號”即將建成交付；全球最大27.1萬立方米LNG船在我國開工建造；16家中國車企旗下25個品牌的新汽車產品亮相剛剛閉幕的巴黎車展。

三是製造“智”，生產模式加快重構。“人工智慧+製造”專項行動深入實施，我國自研AI全直驅數控機床實現突破，整機綜合效能較國家標準提升30%至60%；已累計建成1.3萬餘家先進級、500餘家卓越級智慧工廠，培育15家領航級智慧工廠。企業加快探索工程機械共享製造、汽車島式製造、家電敏捷定製等未來製造模式。（央視新聞）

Other notable decliners included Safran (-1.5%), Legrand (-3.7%) and Société Générale (-0.9%). ### 2026-10-20 15:43:07  #Trading Economics #Economy #Poland #Producer Prices Change #Importance 1

Poland Producer Inflation Tops Forecasts — Producer prices in Poland rose by 6.1% year-on-year in September 2026, exceeding market expectations for a 5.8% gain and accelerating from an upwardly revised 4.8% increase in the previous month. This marked the seventh consecutive month of producer price growth and the highest reading since April 2023, driven by higher prices in all the major sectors, such as mining and quarrying (7.8% vs 6.8% in August), manufacturing (6.4% vs 5.1%), and electricity, gas steam and air conditioning (4.2% vs 2.7%). Meanwhile, producer inflation remained steady at 2.1% for water supply; sewerage, waste management and remediation activities. On a monthly basis, producer prices rose by 0.7% in September, easing from an upwardly revised 1.1% gain in the preceding period. For the January to September period, producer prices were 1.9% higher compared to a year ago.

### 2026-10-20 15:31:55  #宏觀 #焦點

【加快發展新一代智慧製造 工信部明確四方面重點舉措】國務院新聞辦公室今日（10月20日）舉行新聞釋出會。

會上介紹，黨中央、國務院高度重視新一代智慧製造發展。今年9月召開的全國先進製造業大會明確提出，要以新一代智慧製造為主攻方向，加快推動製造業智慧化、綠色化、融合化發展。目前，工業和資訊化部正在研究起草新一代智慧製造的有關政策措施，加快推動人工智慧與製造業深度融合。

發展新一代智慧製造，關鍵在於推動人工智慧技術與製造業全鏈條深度融合，提升裝備和系統的自感知、自決策和自執行能力，促進生產方式和企業組織形態變革，探索形成新的製造模式，幫助企業降低成本、提升效率，更好滿足多樣化市場需求。工業和資訊化部將充分發揮我國人工智慧創新活躍和製造業場景豐富的獨特優勢，重點做好四方面工作。

一是梯度培育智慧工廠。
鼓勵基礎級、先進級智慧工廠夯實數字化、網路化基礎，在研發設計、生產製造、運營管理、售後服務等環節深化人工智慧應用。支援卓越級、領航級智慧工廠探索新一代智慧製造系統架構和未來製造模式，引領行業發展。

二是創新發展智慧裝備。
支援企業研發智慧工業母機、智慧機器人、智慧檢測裝備和智慧控制裝備，提升自適應加工、柔性作業和線上檢測能力，更好滿足高精度、高可靠性生產要求。推動智慧工程機械、智慧農機、智慧醫療裝備等研發應用，提升自主作業和智慧服務能力，讓裝備更加智慧、產品更加好用，支撐相關行業智慧化發展。

三是加快智慧技術攻關和推廣應用。
支援企業圍繞研發設計、生產製造等核心環節，建設高質量工業資料集，開發先進適用的垂域大模型和各類工業專用模型，探索工業智慧作業系統研發，推動資料、模型、軟體和裝備相互適配、高效協同。圍繞智慧排產、引數調優、缺陷檢測、裝置運維等場景，實施模數共振行動，穩妥開展工業智慧體研發與應用驗證，成熟一個、推廣一個。

四是深化智慧製造國際合作。
推動落實智慧製造合作倡議，依託金磚等合作機制，加強智慧工廠建設、標準規範體系等領域合作，鼓勵企業、科研機構分享先進技術、解決方案和建設經驗，開展供需對接和人才培養，協助合作伙伴建設智慧工廠，共同推動製造業轉型升級。（央視新聞）
